Document 010: 10月非农数据点评：就业难唱独角戏

Source PDF: all report pdf/10月非农数据点评：就业难唱独角戏.pdf
Report source: 国信证券
Date: 2026-11-08
Pages: 7
Status: ok

### Page 1

事项：
美东时间 11 月 6 日，美国劳工部统计局（BLS）发布 10 月非农就业报告。新增非农就业 16.5 万人，显
著高于市场预期的 6.5万人；失业率下行至 4.1%，低于市场预期的 4.2%。
评论：
图1：非农数据总览
资料来源：Wind、国信证券经济研究所整理
美国 10 月非农大幅超预期。美国 10 月新增非农就业 16.5 万人，高于预期的 6.5 万人，8-9 月合计上修
8.4万人，显示此前招聘放缓的程度被高估。商务服务、私人教育医疗和运输仓储贡献约九成净新增就业，
咨询、临时用工、医疗及快递招聘构成主要支撑，部分消费服务和地产相关行业仍然承压。我们认为，
美国就业“喜忧参半”，喜的是美国总体就业市场十分强劲，而忧的是就业质量仍存在瑕疵。失业率降至
4.1%，劳动参与率升至 61.9%，就业增长快于劳动力扩张，且裁员压力有限，说明用工需求仍有韧性。不
过，非自愿兼职增加、广义失业率 U6升至 7.9%，表明部分劳动者仍面临工时不足，数据成色有瑕疵。
数据公布后，12 月加息预期升温，但资产表现分化，市场未全面交易持续收紧。站在当前时点，市场对

就业数据的反应存在两难：过弱容易引发衰退交易，过强又会增加激进加息的担忧。本月数据明显偏强，
2026年11月08日

### Page 2

缓解了增长担忧，也使政策收紧重新成为市场关注点。不过，从盘中表现看，其影响主要集中于强化 12
月加息预期（12 月加息 25 个基点的概率升至 76.8%），尚未体现为对明年更大幅度收紧的全面定价。美
债收益率冲高后，长端回吐涨幅，短端仍高于公布前水平；美元走强、黄金下跌，美股开盘后反弹但随
后有所回落。就业韧性缓解了增长担忧，同时增加了利率对资产估值的约束。
这份非农是否属于沃什在 10 月会议上所说的“需要等待的更多信息”？我们认为，确实如此。就业回暖
印证了经济韧性，减轻了进一步收紧的就业顾虑，也增加了 12 月加息的理由。但它是否足以改变政策路
径，成为决定后续加息幅度的关键变量？我们认为，尚不足以。一是非农数据修订较大，单月改善仍需
多项指标相互印证，或连续数月形成趋势；二是在就业保持韧性、价格压力尚未消退的背景下，通胀仍
是政策权衡的重点，就业改善提供了加息空间，却不能单独决定加息幅度；三是对市场而言，民主党掌
控国会后的财政政策变化、AI 投资及回报前景，会更直接地影响中期增长和利率预期，其重要性超过单

月宏观数据。我们维持 12 月加息 25个基点、明年再加息一次的基准判断。
 新增非农：远超预期，招聘改善仍有结构分化
10 月新增非农就业 16.5 万人，显著高于 6.5 万人的市场预期，且 8-9 月合计上修 8.4 万人，显示招聘
修复强于此前判断。我们认为，企业经营活动扩张与用工需求回补是招聘改善的重要支撑，制造业和服
务业 ISM就业指数均处于扩张区间，也提供了旁证。此外，年末旺季招聘为就业提供有力支撑。
从结构看，医疗、商务服务和物流主导本月就业增长，但行业改善仍不均衡。10 月私营部门新增就业
15.9 万人，其中服务业增加 14.4 万人，商品生产增加 1.5万人；政府部门增加 0.6万人。专业商业服务
增加 7.0万人，主要由咨询和临时用工带动；私人教育医疗增加 5.3万人，其中医疗保健增加 4.16 万人，
医院及居家医疗是主要来源；运输仓储增加 2.48 万人，主要由快递招聘支撑，仓储就业则略有减少。上
述三个行业合计贡献约九成净新增就业，而休闲酒店、批发和建筑分别减少 1.3万、0.91万和 0.3万人，

表明招聘修复尚未覆盖全部消费服务及地产相关环节。
图2：新增非农就业趋势 图3：新增非农就业季节性因素

 失业率：就业吸纳改善，广义失业率逆势上升
10 月就业吸纳改善，失业率小幅下降。失业率由 4.2%降至 4.1%，劳动参与率同步升至 61.9%，说明更多
人进入求职市场后，就业增长仍能消化新增供给。从绝对就业人数上看，就业增长快于劳动力增长。10
月劳动力增加 42.1万人，就业增加 46.3万人，失业人数减少 4.1 万人，就业人口比升至 59.3%。我们认
为，失业率回落伴随着参与率和就业人口比同步上升，反映新增劳动力得到更充分吸收。
不过，广义失业率 U6 由 7.6%升至 7.9%，表明岗位数量改善的同时，部分劳动者仍面临工时不足，就业
修复的质量弱于总量表现。失业者分项中，职位流失者及临时工作结束者合计减少 3.4 万人，其中临时
裁员人数减少 7.9 万人、永久职位流失者减少 1.7 万人，临时工作结束者增加 6.2 万人。再进入者增加

0.8万人、新进入者减少 4.0万人，可见新增求职者积压有所缓解。
我们认为，从 10月非农数据来看，美国就业喜忧参半。喜的是新增就业回升、失业率下降，且裁员压力
有限，用工需求仍有韧性；忧的是非自愿兼职增加、广义失业率上升，就业质量改善滞后于数量增长。
往后看，我们更关注国会易手后的预算拨款和政府采购。若两党分歧拖慢项目审批与资金到位，依赖公
共项目的工程建设、科研及专业服务企业将承受订单兑现压力，招聘与就业也将承压。
图6：美国失业率 图7：美国劳动参与率与新增登记失业人数

 往后看：近期加息预期强化，中期路径仍取决于通胀
非农数据公布后，市场上调 12 月加息预期。12 月加息 25 个基点的概率升至 76.8%，较前一日提高约 10
个百分点，表明就业回暖增强了市场对联储继续收紧的判断。2027 年仍定价加息一次，市场对本轮加息
的终点仍未形成一致预期。资产价格先交易加息预期升温，随后出现分化，强非农尚未推动远期利率全
面上行。数据发布后 1 小时，2 年期美债收益率上行约 2.9个基点，10 年和 30 年期收益率则在冲高后分
别回落约 0.4 和 2.3 个基点；美元小幅走强，黄金下跌约 34 美元。纳指和标普 500 开盘后一度较开盘上
涨约 0.7%和 0.8%，随后回吐部分涨幅。
我们认为，此次非农对市场的直接影响有限，但确实是沃什等待的重要数据之一。就业回暖进一步印证
经济韧性，使联储能够更多关注通胀，也增加了 12 月加息的理由。不过，油价回落并不意味着通胀压力

已经消退，核心商品和服务价格仍有黏性。对市场而言，国会易手后的财政政策走向、AI 投资能否持续，
是比单月非农更重要的中期主线。这些变化会先影响市场对未来增长和利率的判断，再逐步体现在就业
等宏观数据中。
我们延续先前判断，预计 12 月加息 25 个基点，明年加息一次。我们判断，油价回落将逐步缓解整体通
胀压力，但核心商品成本传导尚未结束，服务价格也仍有黏性，即便能源降温难以成为美联储立即停止
加息的充分理由。当前就业韧性减轻了联储对进一步收紧冲击经济的顾虑，使其能够更多关注价格稳定。
我们暂以 12 月和明年各加息一次为基准，是否进一步收紧，仍取决于价格压力消退的程度。
图10：CME利率期货数据

2  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/
上週重要市場資訊
 澳洲 8 月就業人數勁增 3.95 萬人大幅優於預期，但勞動參與率上升至 67.1%，
推升失業率至 4.6%，呈現勞動力擴增但市場邊際降溫跡象。澳洲央行總裁布拉
克日前指出，失業率回升至5%有助於抑制頑固通膨。考量物價長期高於目標中
點，市場預期澳洲央行本週二將重啟緊縮，升息 1 碼至 4.60%，創 2011年底以
來新高。
 瑞士央行（SNB）上週四宣布連續第五次將關鍵政策利率維持在0%不變，符合
市場預期。儘管受油價推升使 8 月通膨微升至 0.8%，但仍處於目標區間，央行
預期中期通膨壓力溫和，現行極度寬鬆立場依然適當。決議公布後瑞郎兌美元跌
至 1.2077。相較全球主要央行相繼緊縮，瑞士央行維持超低利率，策略轉為視情
況調節匯市以穩定瑞郎。
 標普全球公布歐元區 9 月綜合 PMI 初值升至 53.1，創三年新高，製造與服務業
同步擴張，新訂單增速達四年多最快，印證經濟無懼美伊衝突引發的能源成本飆

升，展現超預期韌性。受能源價格傳導加速影響，通膨壓力再度升溫，儘管尚未
浮現薪資二輪效應，但強勁景氣數據促使市場大幅提高歐洲央行提前於10月例
會再度升息的押注。
 美國 9 月綜合 PMI 初值勁升至 58.4，創 62 個月新高；製造業與服務業 PMI 亦
逆勢走強至 57與 58.7，凸顯企業擴張強勁，引發市場對通膨持續性的擔憂。市
場焦點轉向 9 月 30日公布的美國 8 月 PCE、個人消費支出與 Q2 GDP終值，同
日日本亦將發布短觀調查與央行意見摘要。若終端消費延續強勁，將牽動美日央
行政策利率預期。

### Page 3

3  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
 美元兌新台幣匯率走勢  
Sep/29/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
上週台幣匯價高點在 31.655，低點在 31.866，以 31.790作收，較前一周收盤價
升值 2.8分。 
上週台幣先升後貶，周初，台股創新高，外資連續五日買超台股並匯入，推升
台幣最高來到 31.655，不過逢低吸引進口商、政府基金等買盤進場，台幣主要在
31.700 附近震盪；臨近周末，美國 9 月綜合 PMI 由 8 月的 56 走升至 58.4，創下逾
5 年來的新高，加上美伊戰事持續，皆推升市場對後續升息的預期，美元指數升至
101 上方，台幣跳空開低，回貶至 31.800 上方，最低來到 31.866，不過隨後出口商
賣壓出籠，台幣跌勢放緩，最終在央行調節之下收在 31.790。 
＜未來分析＞ 
     預估本週台幣區間震盪。就國際市場而言，美國經濟表現仍具韌性，9月綜合
PMI 由 8 月的 56 上升至 58.4，企業活動加速至逾 5 年來最強，數據公布後美國 10
年債券殖利率升破 5%，Fed Watch 顯示市場對於聯準會 10 月升息預期升高，有助
於美元指數維持偏強格局，預計將給台幣帶來壓力；就台灣市場來看，台股在創下
新高後回落，而外資也結束連續五日買超的走勢，轉為賣超，短線上資金匯入不利
台幣表現，然而，台灣8 月外銷訂單達 1,029.6億美元、年增 71.4%，創歷史新高，
AI 需求帶動出口持續強勁，出口商後續拋匯需求預計將提供台幣支撐。綜合以上，
預估本週台幣區間震盪。

強烈鷹派信號，聖路易聯儲總裁穆薩萊姆直言當前 3.75%-4.00%利率仍偏寬鬆，呼
籲盡早收緊；芝加哥聯儲總裁古爾斯比亦警告，AI投資繁榮引發需求過熱，疊加持
續性供應衝擊，可能迫使美聯儲加速且更積極地升息。目前Fedwatch顯示預期明年
4 月前可能再升息三次，將不利歐元表現。三、技術面而言，在日線圖中，EUR/USD

2027 price hike should be case by case amid push back from some customers:
Management said 2027 pricing negotiations could be mixed across customers even
though capacity is nearly fully loaded. Some need more capacity in 4Q26 and are
willing to pay the new post-hike wafer price. However, the company still faces push
back from some customers, likely tied to their inventories. Our recent checks
suggest  Tier 1 foundry L TA wafer pricing into 2027 could be less than 10%, below

the buyside bogey of a 25-30% blended hike. However, for some tier 2 customers,
the price hike could indeed be around 25-30%. We believe the average like-to-like
L TA wafer price could only increase by  15-20% in 2027. Stay EW.
SOI demand exceeds expectation: The company said 12-inch SOI demand already
exceeds its current expansion plan. The priority is to debottleneck and ramp existing
Missouri capacity. More than half of the newly installed tools have qualified for

volume production, and RF SOI and silicon-photonics products have entered volume
production.
4Q26 could be relatively muted: We expect 4Q26 to be relatively muted since
wafer pricing remains tied to older agreements, existing capacity is fully loaded, and
new capacity depreciation would continue to weigh on margin.

Micron/GWC deal marks a new milestone for GWC
Micron Technology and GlobalWafers announced a deeper strategic partnership on July 9,

2026, centered on a 10-year supply agreement and US$500mn of strategic financing from
Micron to support GlobalWafers' new 12-inch silicon wafer facility in Sherman, Texas. On
its latest earnings call, GWC board proposed executing that financing as up to US$500
million of 10-year privately placed convertible bonds, with Micron as the sole subscriber,
carrying a 0.5% coupon. The agreement secures significant raw silicon wafer capacity for
Micron's long-term manufacturing plans, with the financing linked to the buildout of

GlobalWafers America's (GWA) Sherman facility.
GlobalWafers Chairwoman Doris Hsu described the agreement as the largest and longest
contract in the company's history. The two companies will also collaborate on next-
generation wafer technologies and process innovation. The Sherman plant is notable as
the only US-based source of advanced 12-inch wafers.
The agreement forms part of Micron's broader US$3bn commitment to strengthen the US
semiconductor supply chain and accompanies its target of manufacturing 40% of its

DRAM output domestically by the mid-2030s. We believe GWC is well positioned to

### Page 4

M
Idea
4
support ongoing foundry and memory capacity expansion in the US, not only for Micron
but also for TSMC and Samsung, which are already GWC's two largest customers. We
currently assume Micron/GWC L TA pricing could be US$70-80 on average for the polished
wafer (10-20% above current L TA pricing after discounts), reflecting the higher US cost
base. This would be broadly in line with, or slightly above, L TAs signed in 2021-22.
Current inventory levels remain higher than during the 2021-22 upcycle

when existing L TAs were signed
Notably, customer inventories of 12-inch wafers remain well above levels seen during the
previous industry upcycle. According to SUMCO's inventory trend data (see Exhibit 3 ),
inventory volumes and turnover days were near the lowest levels of the 2014-26 period
during 2021-22, reflecting a lean and highly utilized supply chain. By contrast, the current
cycle presents the opposite picture: shipment volumes are approaching record highs, but

customer inventories are also near record levels, with inventory days and absolute
inventory exceeding those seen even at the peak of the 2021-22 upcycle.
This backdrop differs meaningfully from the 2021-22 environment and, in our view,
supports a more moderate and delayed pricing recovery in 2027 rather than a repeat of
the broad-based increases seen during the prior cycle. In 2021-22, wafer makers held
significant pricing leverage as customer inventories sat near cycle lows despite record

shipment volumes. Fabs consumed nearly all purchased wafers with minimal inventory
buffers, creating a tight supply environment that enabled wafer manufacturers to
implement meaningful price increases across 12-inch wafers.
While shipment volumes currently remain elevated, customers also hold substantial
inventory. This provides a buffer against demand growth and moderate supply disruptions,
reducing the urgency to secure incremental wafer supply. Historically, this dynamic

weakens suppliers' pricing power. Until inventories normalize meaningfully, wafer makers
are likely to have less pricing leverage than they did entering the 2021-22 upcycle. Our
view is that 2027 L TA negotiations will focus primarily on removing current discounts
rather than driving nominal price increases. We broadly expect 2027 industry L TA pricing
increases of 15-20% in average, with Tier 2 customers' wafer price to be raised 25-30% but
Tier 1 customers would only see 5-15%.

Valuation Methodology
We lower our price target from NT$900 to NT$770: The price target change mostly
reflects our earnings forecast change over  2026-28. All of of key assumptions in our DCF
model are unchanged, including  a cost of equity of 11% (beta of 1.5, risk premium of 6%
and risk-free rate of 2%), an intermediate growth rate of 13%  and a terminal growth rate
of 4%.
Our bull- and bear-case values are also lowered, from  NT$1,140.00 to NT$975.00 and NT

$450.00 to NT$385.00, respectively, implying P/Es of 32x and 13x 2027e.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Micron Technology Inc. (MU.O)
30x through-cycle earnings of US $40.00, a premium to history reflecting new opportunities
in AI, in-line with broader semis.
Risks to Upside
n Customers continue to demonstrate an appetite to take on inventory around macroeco-
nomic uncertainty
n Additional wafer intensity of HBM further improves overall supply and demand
n MIcron's HBM share surpasses expectations
Risks to Downside

n Pricing can turn quickly; a falter in end demand with inventories elevated could lead to
a swift price reduction
n HBM demand falters and competition intensifies, pressuring pricing

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Document 118: 261104_daiwa_ Nien Made Enterprise

Source PDF: all report pdf/261104_daiwa_ Nien Made Enterprise.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-11-04
Pages: 7
Status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Consumer Discretionary

What’s new: Nien Made reported its 3Q26 results on 4 November 2026 
after market hours. We reaffirm our Buy (1) call (see our previous flash, 
2Q26 results: record-high gross margin, 12 August 2026, for more 
information).

What’s the impact: 3Q26 results review: slight miss on higher opex. 
Nien Made’s 3Q26 revenue was TWD8,482m (+7.0% QoQ, +15.9% YoY), 
roughly in line with our estimate and the consensus. Its gross margin was 
60.8%, a record-high level, up from 60.0% in 2Q26 and 58.7% in 3Q25, 
beating our estimate (59.6%) and the consensus (59.8%). This was driven 
by price hikes in its custom-made segment and a lower tariff effect, despite 
being partially offset by restocking demand from ready-made products. 
However, its operating margin came in at 29.2%, up from 27.2% in 2Q26 
and the same as in 3Q25, lower than our estimate (30.3%) and the 
consensus (30.1%), due to surging fuel surcharges following oil price hikes 
in 3Q26. As such, operating profit was TWD2,473m (+14.5% QoQ, +15.9% 
YoY), missing our estimate and the consensus by 6.3% and 2.5%, 
respectively. Nien Made’s 3Q26 net profit stood at TWD1,870m (+34.5% 
QoQ, +9.6% YoY), missing our estimate and the Bloomberg consensus by 
8.9% and 4.2%, respectively. Its 3Q26 EPS came in at TWD6.38.

2027: several new initiatives in products and markets. Nien Made is on 
track to meet its 2026 target of growing its custom-made revenue in the US 
by mid-single-digit % YoY, custom-made revenue in non-US markets by 
high-single-digit % YoY and ready-made revenue by low-single-digit % YoY. 
Besides, Nien Made will continue to launch new products such as “tool-
free, drill-free” easy-install products and new blind programmes, and 
upgrade its motorised window coverings. Nien Made will continue to invest 
in its window covering brands, channels and service network (ie, European 
markets) as well as manufacturing, which should be the key factor to drive 
more order wins in the long run, in our view. We cut our 2026E EPS by 3%, 
after factoring in the 3Q26 results and keep our 2027-28E EPS almost 
unchanged.

What we recommend: We reaffirm our Buy (1) call on Nien Made and 
raise our 12M TP to TWD520 (from TWD515), still based on a target PER 
of 20x on our 1-year forward EPS vs. its past 3-year PER trading range of 
11-21x. Key downside risks: lower-than-expected revenue from custom-
made products and macro/tariff uncertainties.

How we differ: We are more positive on the company’s leading position 
with share-gain potential. Nien Made is one of our preferred stocks in 
Taiwan’s non-tech sector.

4 November 2026

3Q26 results: record-high gross margin

➢ Despite record-high gross margin, 3Q26 net profit missed on opex 
➢ 2027: ongoing product mix optimisation trend  
➢ Reaffirming our Buy (1) rating; raising 12M TP to TWD520

Company profile 
Founded in July 2007 and listed on Taiwan’s main board in December 2015, Nien Made Enterprise 
(Nien Made) produces window coverings such as venetian blinds, shutters and shades (ie, cellular 
shades, roller blinds and roman blinds). Its major clients include dealers, designers and small specialty 
stores as well as big box retailers (ie, Home Depot and Wal-Mart).

### Page 5

5

Nien Made Enterprise (8464 TT): 4 November 2026 
ESG analysis

Update Date: 24 Jun 2026 
Source: Daiwa, Company

ESG risks
Risks Management Analyst comments
G
Executive/board
quality 2
Its CEO and Chairman is the same person. Also, 3 brothers, with 1 being the CEO (Chairman)
and 2 being Vice President, are in charge of different divisions, managing and collaborating
collectively, which we view as a positive sign for solid corporate governance.
Capital management 2
Nien Made's dividend payout ratio was c.80% before 2019, but dropped to 52-71% from 2019 to
2025, which we attribute to its capacity expansion plans in Mexico and some adjustment for
COVID-19.
Related party &
transaction 2
Revenue from related-party transactions stood at c.60% of total revenue over 2022-25, with
majority from Norman International Inc. and its subsidiary. We see no evidence of suspicious
activities.
E Product design & lifecycle management 1
We note Nien Made has improved its quality, safety and efficiency throughout its product
lifecycle. The company has developed its own B2B platform for its customers for a better user
experience and efficiency. Nien Made's products have a lifespan ranging from 10 to 20 years.
Therefore, in addition to controlling production conditions through equipment such as drying kilns
and controlled-environment storage, Nien Made also focus on mitigating consumers’ health and
safety risks.
S Supply chain management 2
Nien Made's overall local-to-local raw material sourcing was at 71% in 2023, which we believe
will help reduce transportation costs in the long term. Moreover, Nien Made established supplier
management policies and relevant regulations, and its 234 suppliers signed the Supplier Code of
Conduct as of 2023.
Note: Management score represents a company's ability to manage/benefit from certain ESG topics. The scores range from 1 to 3, with 1 being the strongest.

### Page 1

Summary: SDI reported its 3Q26 results after market hours on 5 November. Its 3Q26 EPS 
was TWD1.94, beating our EPS estimate (TWD1.79) but missing the Bloomberg 
consensus (TWD2.07).

We have a Buy (1) rating on SDI with a 12-month TP of TWD265, based on a PER of 35x 
on our 1-year-forward EPS. For more information on the company, please refer to our 
latest flash, 2Q26 review: net profit beat expectations, on 7 August 2026.

What’s the impact 
• 3Q26 results beat our estimate but missed the Bloomberg consensus. SDI’s 3Q26 
revenue of TWD4,020m (+12.9% QoQ, +58.1% YoY) beat our and the consensus 
estimates by 12.4% and 2.4%, mainly due to the better-than-expected auto segment 
and rush orders for the consumer segment. Its gross margin came in at 20.5% in 3Q26 
(vs. 20.7% in 2Q26 and 12.4% in 3Q25), missing both our (20.7%) and the consensus 
(21.5%) estimates, which we attribute to a lower-than-expected yield rate and OT 
expense despite positive factors such as price hikes, a higher copper price and FX. Its 
operating profit of TWD517m (+24.0% QoQ, +399.0% YoY) beat our estimate by 13.4%, 
but was in line with the consensus estimate. Its net profit came in at TWD383m, which 
also beat our estimate (TWD339m) but missed the Bloomberg consensus (TWD400m). 
Its 3Q26 EPS was TWD1.94. For 3Q26 revenue, electronics accounted for 91% 
(+14.2% QoQ, +67.3% YoY) and stationery accounted for 9% (+1.6% QoQ, +1.6% YoY). 
Within electronics, the product mix was: auto 47% (+14.2% QoQ, +63.8% YoY), 
industrial 15% (+14.2% QoQ, +19.5% YoY), consumer 28% (+6.6% QoQ, +67.3% YoY), 
AI lead frame 7% (+33.2% QoQ) and others 3% (+71.3% QoQ, +67.3% YoY).

• Bright lead frame outlook with rising AI lead frame. SDI expects 4Q26 revenue to be 
flat to slightly up QoQ and 20%+ revenue YoY growth for its 2027 lead frame business, 
due to an intact order outlook from auto, industrial and AI. It also expects its AI lead 
frame revenue is to reach 10%+ of 2027 revenue.

What we recommend 
We have a Buy (1) rating on SDI with a 12-month TP of TWD265, based on a PER of 35x 
on our 1-year-forward EPS. The stock is currently trading at PERs of 25.2x/19.1x, based 
on our 2026/27E EPS, vs. its past-3-year one-year forward PER trading range of 12-72x

Aspeed (5274.TWO): Servers upcycle to continue driving growth; 3Q26 
GM in line; Buy
6 November 2026 | 8:29PM HKT
Oct revenues in line: Oct revenues +24% MoM (vs. 3Q26 revenues +24% QoQ), 
accounting for 31% of our 4Q26E revenue forecast, largely in line. Looking into 
4Q26, we expect revenue to grow 38% QoQ on robust demand, with GM rebounding 
to 71.9% driven by both product mix upgrade and higher price, reflecting the strong 
demand across general servers and AI servers. For 2027, we expect both AI and 
general servers to drive growth, with customer order visibility now extending into 
2Q27, while a rising AST2700 contribution and the price hike should further lift 
blended ASP . 3Q26 in line: GM declined sequentially on continued material cost 
increases, and lands at 69.5%, in line with our estimates. Opex ratio of 17.3% came in 
above GSe/cons of 15.4%/15.2%, leading operating income to +20% QoQ, or 3% / 
1% behind our / Bloomberg consensus estimates, largely in line. We maintain our 
12m TP at NT$22,000 (implied 53x 2027E PE vs. 80% NI YoY of 2027-28E on avg.). 
Maintain Buy.

We expect 4Q26E revenue to grow at 38% QoQ, driven by BMC demand, a rising 
AST2700 mix, easing supply bottlenecks as a new OSAT partner is introduced in 
4Q26, and another round of price adjustments to pass on higher material costs. Our 
forecast implies average monthly revenue of NT$2.3bn in November-December 
(+13% vs. October). On the margin front, we expect higher pricing to more than 
offset rising material costs, driving a sequential GM rebound in 4Q26E. For 
2027-28E, we expect Aspeed to be driven by 1) robust agentic AI demand, which we 
expect to drive continued growth in AI-related general server BMC shipments, 
supported by solid CPU demand; 2) accelerating AI ASIC deployments, with

### Page 4

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Aspeed (5274.TWO) 
Valuation: Our 12-month TP of NT$22,000 is based on a target P/E of 40x (in line with 
its 10-year historical trading average) applied to our 2028E EPS, and discounted back to 
2027E at 13.6% CoE. Our key assumptions for CoE include: (1) 1.5x beta (sourcing 
Bloomberg), (2) a 4.25% risk-free rate, and (3) a market risk premium at 6.25% (in line 
with GS house view). 
Key downside risks to our view and TP: 1) A softer-than-expected server market 
demand recovery, 2) slower-than-expected BMC penetration within AI servers, 3) 
intensifying market competition.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.
Coverage Universe: A list of all stocks in each coverage universe is available by primary analyst, stock and coverage universe at

https://www.gs.com/research/hedge.html.

Update
Lite-On Technology  | Asia Pacific
October  Sales +6% MoM/+32%
YoY
In this report, we focus on Lite-On's monthly sales, which we
believe could be a catalyst for its share price.
Details:
• October sales came in at NT$20,071mn (+6% MoM/+32% YoY), driven by the
server PSU/BBU and opto semiconductors (couplers and indicators for
industrial automation, new energy and AI applications).
• QTD revenue reached 33% of both MSe at NT$60,395mn (+5% QoQ/+36%

YoY), and consensus at NT$60,353mn (+5% QoQ/+36% YoY).
• By segment, Cloud & AIoT/Optoelectronics/IT&CE accounted for
66%/13%/21% of the monthly sales, respectively, implying growth of +19%/-
1%/-17% MoM and  +71%/+7%/-16% YoY.
Our view:
• We think its 4Q26 revenue is tracking slightly ahead of market expectations.
• We believe Lite-On will grow along with the AI capex cycle driven by its
power-related offerings, though execution and progress with new customers

remain key to watch.
• Stay EW.

ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

### Page 1

2026.10.30   
한화에어로스페이스  
(012450)

3Q26: 그래도 믿는다  Company Brief

3분기 매출 6조 943억원/영업이익 4,520억원 
한화에어로스페이스의 3분기 매출은 6조 943억원/영업이익 4,520억원(OPM 7.4%)로 
컨센서스와 당사 추정치를 모두 대폭 하회하는 실적을 기록하였다. 앞서 실적을 발표한 
한화오션의 실적 쇼크를 감안하더라도, 주력사업인 지상방산 부문의 실적이 매출 1조 
2,455억원/영업이익 217억원(1.7%)로 지난 분기(2조 1,075억원/ 5,330억원/ 25.3%) 
는 물론 전년동기(2조 1,098억원/5,725억원/27.1%)보다도 현저히 감소하였다. 
일회성 요인은 대전사업장 폭발사고에 따른 복구비용 등 300억원, 일부 국내사업 
충당금 160억원 및 환율하락에 따른 효과 등이 있었으나 역시 이를 감안해도 내수와 
수출 모두 실질 이익률은 5%미만으로 추정된다. 지상방산 분기 매출은 1분기(1조 
2,211억)보다 오히려 소폭 증가하였으나 영업이익이 1분기 2,087억원에서 
217억원으로 대폭 감소한 점에 대하여 회사는 프로젝트별 믹스 구성에 따른 낮은 
이익률의 인도분이 반영되었다고 설명하고 있으나, 그럼에도 불구하고 수출과 내수 
이익률 모두 BEP에 가까웠다는 점은 쉽게 이해가 가지는 않는다. 다만 방산기업의 
특성상 대전사업장 사고에 따른 실제 납기 영향 등 회사가 공개할 수 없는 사정이 
있었을 것으로 추측할 뿐이다. 대전사업장은 현재 복구작업이 진행 중이며 27년 
상반기 중에는 가동 정상화할 것으로 예상된다.  
긍정적인 소식도 있다. 폴란드향 천무의 연간 인도대수가 기존 가이던스인 40대에서 
60대로 증가하였으며, 이를 포함하여 3분기에 인도되지 않은 물량의 대부분이 
4분기에 집중될 예정으로, 올해의 4분기 실적 개선폭은 통상적으로 볼 수 있는 
4분기의 계절적 집중도보다 더 커질 것으로 예상한다. 방산기업의 실적은 인도일정에 
따라 크게 변할 수 있는 분기 실적보다 연간 합산이 더 중요하다는 점을 한 번 더 
믿고, 4분기를 기다리는 게 좋겠다.

투자의견 BUY 유지, 목표주가 1,200,000원 하향  
투자의견 BUY를 유지하지만, SOTP기반 목표주가는 기존 1,490,000원에서 
1,200,000원으로 19.5% 하향한다. 비영업가치 중 자회사 한화시스템과 한화오션, 
쎄트렉아이의 지분가치 하락(합산 8.7조원→7.1조원), 그리고 지난 분기 대비 늘어난 
별도 순차입금(3.25조→4.95조원)을 반영하였으며 최근 방산섹터 전반의 밸류 하락을 
감안하여 영업가치 중 지상방산+항공우주의 Target P/E를 기존 35배에서 30배로 
조정하였다. 
3분기는 여러모로 악재가 겹쳤다. 주식시장 전반의 하락에 더해 방산섹터 수주 지연에 
따른 디레이팅, 그리고 납기 지연에 따른 실적 하락 등 나올 수 있는 악재는 다 
나왔다고도 볼 수 있다. 그러나 뒤집어보면 지연중인 수주는 시간의 문제일 뿐 가시화 
가능성이 매우 높으며, 실적 또한 진행률 인식이 아닌 인도기준 인식에 따른 일시적 
현상으로 4분기 이후 복구될 가능성이 크다. 펀더멘털의 유의미한 변화는 없다고 볼 
수 있으며 밸류에이션 매력도가 증가한 현 시점에서 추가 매수를 고려할 때다. 다만, 
대전사업장 사고에 따른 당국의 제재조치가 아직 확정되지 않았음은 잔여 리스크 
요인이다. 동종사의 신규계약 입찰제한 제재조치가 주가에 상당한 영향을 미쳤음을 
고려하면 한화에어로스페이스 또한 제재조치 여부를 지켜본 후 투자할 것을 권한다.

Compliance notice 
당 보고서 공표일 기준으로 해당 기업과 관련하여, 
 회사는 해당 종목을 1%이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다.  
 금융투자분석사와 그 배우자는 해당 기업의 주식을 보유하고 있지 않습니다.  
 당 보고서는 기관투자가 및 제 3자에게 E-mail등을 통하여 사전에 배포된 사실이 없습니다.  
 회사는 6개월간 해당 기업의 유가증권 발행과 관련 주관사로 참여하지 않았습니다. 
 당 보고서에 게재된 내용들은 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다. 
 당 연구원은 한화에어로스페이스(012450)의 일부 비용을 지원받아 한화에어로스페이스 공장 투어(2026년 10월 1-2)를 다녀왔습니다. 
본 분석자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위한 참고자료이며, 따라서, 본 자료에 의한 투자자의 투자결과에 대해 어떠한 목적의 증빙자료로도 사용될 수 없으며, 어떠한 경우에도 작성자 및 
당사의 허가 없이 전재, 복사 또는 대여될 수 없습니다. 무단전재 등으로 인한 분쟁발생시 법적 책임이 있음을 주지하시기 바랍니다.

[투자의견] 
종목추천 투자등급

### Page 1

삼성바이오로직스 (207940/KS) 
3Q26 Pre: 파업과 환율 부담에도 컨센 부합 
2026-10-06

제약/바이오. 이선경 / seonkyoung.lee@sks.co.kr / 3773-9089

매수(유지) 
목표주가: 2,000,000 원(유지) 
현재주가: 1,354,000 원 
상승여력: 47.7%

Signal: 파업과 환율 부담에도 컨센 부합 전망  
Key: 5 공장 매출 본격화, 미국 공장 매출 기여에 따른 성장 기대   
Step: 단기 불확실성보다는 중장기적 성장 방향성에 주목

3Q26 Pre: 파업과 환율 부담에도 성장지속, 컨센 부합 전망   
26년 3분기 연결기준 매출액은 1조 3,741억원(+9.3%YoY, +4.0%QoQ), 영업이
익은 6,098억원(-3.7%YoY, +4.0%QoQ, OPM 44.4%)으로 컨센서스 매출액 1
조 3,450억원과 영업이익 6,063억원에 부합하는 실적을 전망한다.  
5월 파업 영향으로 약 20개 배치 생산분(약 1,500억원)에 대한 원가 손실이 인식되
고 3분기 평균 환율이 전분기 대비 약 5% 하락했음에도 불구하고, 3분기부터 매출로 
인식되는 5공장 PPQ 배치 및 미국 공장의 매출 기여로 지속적인 성장이 예상된다. 약 
1,500억원 규모의 생산 차질분은 고객사와 일정 조율을 통해 출하될 예정으로 이에 
따른 4분기 매출 성장을 기대해 볼 수 있다.   
신규 수주 회복과 모달리티 확장에 주목 
시장에서 우려했던 신규 수주는 관세 이슈로 고객사의 컨택이 감소하면서 올해 저조했
으나 4 월 Section 232 결과 발표 이후 논의가 회복 단계에 있어 연내 추가 신규 수주 
가능성은 열려 있다.  
항체 시장의 경쟁심화와 차세대 모달리티 확장에 대응하기 위해 결정한 폴리펩타이드
의 인수는 연내 마무리될 예정이다. 폴리펩타이드의 2028 년 목표 EBITDA 마진은 
25%로 동사 대비 낮은 상황이나, 펩타이드 기반 CDMO 시장 성장에 따른 마진 개선
도 가능하다. 현재 임단협, 환율, 미국 공장 및 폴리펩타이드 인수에 따른 회계 영향 등 
불확실성이 큰 상황이나, 수주 회복과 5 공장 매출 인식 본격화, 미국 공장 및 멀티 모
달리티 확장 등 중장기 성장 방향성에 주목할 필요가 있다.

TUẦN 02-06/11 [Điểm nhấn thị trường] 
VNINDEX giảm điểm trong tuần qua, -16.26 điểm tương ứng -0.93%, với thanh 
khoản mở rộng

[Nhận định xu hướng] 
Diễn biến phân hóa và bi ến động mạnh có thể còn tiếp diễn, tuy nhiên khi xu 
hướng tăng ngắn hạn vẫn đang được bảo lưu, chỉ số được kỳ vọng sẽ tiếp tục 
hướng lên thử thách lại các vùng cản phía trên

### Page 2

2

Điểm nhấn thị trường 
Chỉ số, nhóm ngành, khối ngoại và phái sinh

Top 5 ngành tăng / giảm  VNINDEX giảm điểm trong tuần qua, -16.26 điểm tương ứng -0.93%, với thanh khoản mở 
rộng. Thiết bị dụng cụ Y tế, Tiện ích khí và Bảo hiểm là các nhóm ngành có mức giảm rõ rệt 
nhất trong tuần qua. Khối ngoại duy trì bán ròng so với xu hướng của tuần trước với giá trị 
-870 tỷ đồng, tập trung ở các mã MBB, VIC và VCB; mặt khác, tự doanh đảo chiều mua ròng 
ở mức 504 tỷ đồng.

Ở một khía cạnh khác, khối ngoại duy trì bán ròng -2,010 HĐTL VN30F1M trong tuần qua 
và đang duy trì vị thế bán lũy kế -10,888 hợp đồng. Tự doanh duy trì mua ròng 1,208 hợp 
đồng nhưng đang duy trì trạng thái lũy kế bán ròng -4,070 hợp đồng.

TTCK Việt Nam có tuần giảm điểm thứ 2 liên tiếp khi VN-Index lùi về kiểm định vùng hỗ trợ 
quanh 1.725 điểm, với phiên cuối tuần có thời điểm giảm hơn 30 điểm trước khi thu hẹp 
đà giảm nhờ lực cầu ở nhóm chứng khoán. Áp lực bán tập trung ở nhóm vốn hóa lớn, đặc 
biệt là bộ đôi Vingroup (VIC, VHM) cùng một số cổ phiếu ngân hàng và công nghệ, khiến 
chỉ số thiếu lực đỡ. Giao dịch của khối ngoại diễn biến trái chiều và giằng co — có phiên 
mua ròng trở lại ở một số mã, nhưng vẫn xen kẽ bán ròng ở nhóm trụ như VHM, VCB, cho 
thấy dòng vốn ngoại chưa xác lập xu hướng rõ ràng. Bối cảnh bên ngoài kém thuận lợi khi 
nhóm cổ phiếu công nghệ tại Mỹ bị bán tháo, kéo theo các chỉ số chính toàn cầu đồng 
loạt lao dốc. Mặc dù vậy, kỳ vọng vào động thái mở kênh hoán đổi ngoại tệ của NHNN để 
hỗ trợ thanh khoản, hạ lãi suất đã giúp tâm lý nhà đầu tư trong nước ổn định và không 
xuất hiện áp lực bán tháo.

Quan điểm kỹ thuật 
Xu hướng & Hành động

Chỉ số VNINDEX

Source: Trading View, KB Securities Vietnam

Ngắn hạn 
(Dưới 3 tháng)

Trung hạn (Từ 3 đến 9 tháng)

Chỉ báo  Nhận định xu hướng Hành động 
Xanh đậm  Khả năng tăng điểm cao Mua mạnh 
Xanh nhạt  Khả năng tăng điểm vừa phải Mua từng phần 
Vàng  Xu hướng trung tính Mua/Bán trading 
Cam  Khả năng giảm điểm vừa phải Bán từng phần 
Đỏ  Khả năng giảm điểm cao Bán mạnh

Ngưỡng kỹ thuật

VNINDEX tuần qua hình thành mẫu nến spinning thân nhỏ với bóng trên/dưới khá dài, 
cùng thanh khoản gia tăng, thể hiện sự giằng co mạnh nhưng về tổng thể lại cho thấy diễn 
biến đã cân bằng trở lại sau tuần giảm điểm trước đó. Trên khung ngày, mặc dù trải qua 
các phiên biến động tăng/giảm mạnh đan xen nhưng việc cho phản ứng bật tăng tích cực 
tại quanh đường biên dưới của mẫu hình tam giác đi ngang trung hạn cho thấy tương quan 
vẫn đang nghiêng về chiều hướng tăng. Diễn biến phân hóa và biến động mạnh có thể còn 
tiếp diễn, tuy nhiên khi xu hướng tăng ngắn hạn vẫn đang được bảo lưu, chỉ số được kỳ 
vọng sẽ tiếp tục hướng lên thử thách lại các vùng cản phía trên.

(Ghi chú: NĐT có thể dựa theo nhận định xu hướng thị trường để tham khảo khuyến nghị 
hành động tương ứng trong bảng phía trên) 
Kháng cự xa: 1800 (+-15) 
Kháng cự gần: 1750 (+-10) 
Hỗ trợ gần: 1700 (+-10) 
Hỗ trợ xa: 1660 (+-15)

### Page 4

4

Danh mục đầu tư mẫu

Phương pháp tiếp cận: 
- Danh mục tập hợp các cổ phiếu tiềm năng nhất được KBSV 
lựa chọn và tái cơ cấu theo tháng. 
- Ngưỡng cắt lỗ cần được cân nhắc cho mỗi mã là -15%. 
- Danh mục giả định luôn duy trì tỷ trọng 100% là cổ phiếu với 
trọng số được phân bổ đều giữa các mã.

Lưu ý:  
- NĐT được khuyến nghị mua tại mức giá bằng hoặc thấp hơn 
giá khuyến nghị mua. 
- Lợi nhuận kỳ vọng được tính theo giá khuyến nghị mua.

Document 335: GFHK - Apple F4Q26 review

### Page 1

Muted iPhone 18 Pro Cycle and Rising Memory Costs

[Table_Rank]

Maintained  Hold  
 Target price US$334

[Table_Summary] Lower TP to $334, Hold rating: Driven by investors being away from AI themes, Apple share 
price outperformed Nasdaq by 7.2% in the past one month. However, we continue to expect 
muted cycle of iPhone 18 P/PM with constrained volume of iPhone Duo. Additionally, memory 
cost hike will last longer than expected, driven by our view of tight DRAM/NAND supply, and 
we expect cost hikes throughout CY2027. For hardware, we still expect the iPhone 20 (2H27) 
to be a strong product cycle, thanks to new design of display, followed by Apple’s early move 
to TSMC ’s A14 in 2H28 (suggesting only 2 -years at 2nm). Additionally, Apple Intelligence 
remains the key to watch. Yet, the share price trades at 35x FY27E P/E, suggesting the stock 
isn’t cheap. By factoring in results, guidance and lower shipments/margins, we revise FY27E 
EPS by -10% and revise TP from $369 to $334, still based on 35x FY27E. 
Dec-Q margin guidance below expect ations: Apple delivered solid Sep t-Q results, with 
revenue of $114.5bn (+12% YoY) and diluted EPS of $2.1 (+14% YoY), both ahead of 
consensus. For Dec-Q, mgmt. guided to total revenue growth of 7 –10% YoY (vs. consensus 
of +6.5%), Services growth of 8 –9%, and gross margin of 45 –46%. The revenue outlook is 
constructive to Street expectations, though the margin guidance and Services deceleration 
were disappointing, reflecting cost hikes and mix headwinds.  
The iPhone product cycle mixed, with challenges in FY2027: Mgmt sees a strong start of 
iPhone Duo but remains supply constrained. Hence, we continue to expect the EMS build plan 
of 6.5m of iPhone Duo by end-2026 with limited upside, while the mainstream iPhone 18 P/PM 
to be 74.5m. As said in our earlier report of iPhone 18 Pro/Pro Max Lukewarm on Sept 15, we 
believe the muted demand will weigh on CY1H27 EMS builds. Margin wise, despite p ricing 
actions taken earlier, margin will likely be under pressure, as the CEO Ternus characterized 
current memory pricing as “unprecedented,” with higher costs in December and beyond.  
Apple positioning the rebuilt Siri AI as a core differentiator: The CEO Ternus emphasized 
that the new Siri is deeply integrated and privacy -preserving and is fielding ~2.5bn requests 
per day. From a financial perspective, CFO Parekh noted that these Si ri AI costs are 
embedded in the December margin outlook, suggesting near-term gross-margin compression 
alongside the memory cost up-cycle.  
Risks: 1) weak demand, 2) competition, 3) product delays, 4) geo-tension.

### Page 2

Earnings Forecast  
Solid September Quarter Results  
Apple delivered a solid September-quarter, with revenue of $114.5bn (+12% YoY) and diluted EPS of 
$2.10 (+14% YoY), both ahead of consensus, reaching a record fiscal year of $479bn in revenue. Gross 
margin came in at 47.9%, aided by approximately one percentage point of tariff refund benefits (and a 
$0.05 EPS tailwind).  
iPhone revenue reached a September-quarter record of $57.6bn (+17.5% YoY) likely driven by higher 
ASPs, Mac rose a strong 24% to $10.8bn likely on edge-AI, Services set at $31.6bn (+9.8% YoY) which 
was below expectations, while iPad declined 10% to $6.2bn and Wearables fell 8% to $8.3bn. The 
company returned $28bn to shareholders. 
December Quarter Guidance Slightly Ahead in Revenue but Shy in Margin  
For the December quarter, mgmt. guided to total revenue growth of 7 –10% YoY ( vs. consensus of 
+6.5%), Services growth of 8–9%, and gross margin of 45–46%. Operating expenses are expected at 
$20.4–20.8bn, OI&E around $300 million, and the tax rate near 17%. The revenue outlook is 
constructive relative to prior Street expectations, though the margin range and Services deceleration 
reflect known cost and mix headwinds.

### Page 5

Valuation and Recommendation  
Hold with TP of $334 
We rate Apple (AAPL US) Hold and set our TP at $334 which is based on 35x our FY27E EPS estimate. 
We believe it is important to capture the 2H26 -3Q27’s iPhone 18 product cycle, given high market 
interest. We adopt P/E methodology as we believe the market now mainly focuses on the company’s 
earnings outlook, and we usually apply P/E method to downstream firms.  
We believe Apple’s ecosystem will remain strong and associated revenue durability is high. This is 
backed by Apple's installed base growth, double digit YoY growth in services. However, the market 
would wait for visibility for its Apple Intelligence. Meanwhile, the market will need more visibility on the 
higher-high retail prices for the iPhone 18 series due to the rising cost over tariff and 2nm/memories. 
That said, we expect its earnings growth to be  high-teens to 20% YoY in FY26E-FY28E, from single 
digit YoY in FY22-FY24. Considering >30x one-year P/E, the stock doesn’t look particularly cheap.

前日收盤價                              76 .6元

GIS-KY(6456 TT)       法說 /座談摘要  
雷射封裝業務進展順利， 2Q27有望顯著放量  
⚫ 公司積極轉型自傳統觸控模組轉向半導體光學  
⚫ 雷射封測試量產進行中，預期年底出貨， 2027將有顯著貢獻  
結論： GIS-KY 3Q26營收有起色，但仍受終端市場需求不振及客戶端記
憶體缺貨的影響，但整體財務結構與淨現金部位 仍穩健。公司目前正積
極推動業務轉型，從傳統觸控模組轉向半導體光學領域，並已擴大資本
支出佈局光通訊雷射光源封測與 AR/VR等新產品，預計這些新事業將於
2027至 2028年成為公司顯著的營收成長動能 。

3Q26財務數據  
⚫ 營收 144億元(QoQ+37.6%，YoY-10.5%)，主要受到終端市場需求
不振及客戶端記憶體缺貨的影響。  
⚫ 毛利率 4.02%(QoQ+0.68ppts，YoY-2.46ppts)  
⚫ 營業費用 12.11億元，前三季單季皆維持在 11.2億元左右的水準。
因公司處於轉型期，於 R&D的投資相對較多。  
⚫ 營業利益 -6.3億元 
⚫ 稅前淨利 -6.69億元 
⚫ 單季 EPS -3.15元 
⚫ 稅後淨利 -7.25億元 
⚫ 單季 EPS -2.17元。 
產品營收占比  
⚫ 3Q26：平板佔 40%、筆電佔 28%、指紋辨識模組佔 13%、其他佔
19%。 
⚫ 1Q-3Q26：平板佔 32%、筆電佔 21%、指紋辨識模組佔 9%、其他
佔 38%。 
法說會內容摘要  
1.  公司中長期轉型策略：半導體光學 (Semi Optics)  
• 光學元件：主要應用於 AR/VR眼鏡、 Waveguide及抬頭顯示器
（HUD） ，涵蓋奈米壓印、蝕刻及鍍膜等製程，目前擁有多個國際大廠
專案。預期 AR/VR軟硬體需至 2027至 2028年才會成熟發酵。  
• 雷射封裝：為近期資本支出重點方向，主力產品為 CW Laser CoC
以及未來的 VCSEL VOD。

### Page 2

【中信投顧投資分析報告】 
個股摘要

202 6 /11 /10

2

• 光通訊元件：發展被動光學元件，不排除整合 FAU、Metalens及上下游模零組件整合。  
• Micro OLED / Micro LED：Micro OLED在顯示器應用發展符合預期，並積極研發 Micro 
LED在光通訊領域的應用。

Q&A內容摘要  
1. Q：新世代半導體光學產品的資本支出目前的廠區規劃及進度 為何？後續大客戶的營
收比重是否會下降？   
A：公告約 80億元的資本支出中，雷射封裝相關預計佔 40至 50億元，約三分之一投入台
灣廠區，其餘投入海外越南新廠。剩下的資本支出將用於 Micro LED、AR/VR波導及大客
戶新產品（廠區分散於大陸及台灣）。與大陸大客戶合作近 20年互信基礎深厚，只要有觸
控與顯示的新產品皆會參與，大客戶仍是重要客戶，但隨新業務逐步發酵，其營收佔比的
確會漸漸下降。

2. Q：10月份營收跳升破百億元的原因？這是一次性的嗎？這是否會佔用過多營運資
金？  
A：10月營收達 130億元，主因為客戶委託進行手機關鍵零組件的加工與精密對位貼合，
這是相對長期的專案，並非一次性。營收跳升主力來自高單價材料，若純看加工費，實際
營收佔比並不高。材料確實會佔用較大營運資金，但因週期短，銀行額度支持無虞；先前
提出的增資案主要是為支撐長期營運資金及較大資本支出。

3. Q：雷射封裝產線何時建好並貢獻營收？ GIS在此領域相對於現有供應商的優勢在
哪？  
A：產線今年 2Q26已陸續建置， 4Q26客戶原材料供應及認證趨於成熟，量產預計在 11月
或 12月從小量往上衝。 GIS的優勢在於：(1)長期與國際大廠合作累積的製造管理 能力。(2)
擁有充足現金流與財務實力，能支撐數十億的資本支出。

### Page 3

【中信投顧投資分析報告】 
個股摘要

202 6 /11 /10

3 
4. Q：雷射出貨的規格？今明兩年的資本支出預估？   
A：目前專注於大於 300mW、400mW的高功率雷射（不涉獵傳統 100mW），主要應用於
ELSFP等 200G以上高階領域，材料主力為磷化銦，同時也開發 CW Laser及 VCSEL封裝。
今年已核准資本支出約 80億元，明年資本支出將視新產品狀況及客戶需求而定，預計今年
底或明年初會較明確。

5. Q：明年筆電與平板的記憶體缺貨狀況會好轉嗎？   
A：據業界交流，因新產能開出尚需時間，記憶體缺貨狀況到 2027年底前改善幅度可能有
限，許多人預估缺貨可能持續到 2028年。

6. Q：FAU的佈局？與集團內的訊芯有無合作？   
A：集團內公司皆獨立營運，訊芯比較接近光模塊加工。 GIS初期不會聚焦整套 FAU模組
組裝，而是先從 passive component（如 Fiber holder、V型槽、透鏡陣列、超透鏡）做起，
未來若元件做得不錯，才會考慮後段的模組。

7. Q：大客戶即將推出搭載 OLED及觸控功能的筆電，貴司是否有參與？  A：不便針對
單一客戶產品發表評論。但只要是分離式的觸控模組（非內嵌式 In-cell Touch） ，公司都會
積極爭取並參與。

8. Q：雷射封裝做到 300mW以上，在技術或設備上有何門檻與優勢？  A：100mW提升
到 300mW或 400mW，封裝的製程流程基本相同，但測試項目及設備必須大幅更新，這也
是新資本支出的重點。基於過去在顯示器背光模組及指紋辨識貼合所累積的光學製程經
驗，跨入此領域對公司來說並非太難。

### Page 4

【中信投顧投資分析報告】 
個股摘要

202 6 /11 /10

4 
9. Q：設備交期似乎很長，何時能滿產？  
A：固晶機、打線機是泛用的，依照現有機台去挪動不難，主要是測試設備（如測試設備
外掛的電流計、頻譜分析儀）交期確實較長，預計 2Q27季底到 3Q27初產能可完全開出。
2Q27量產時預計會有一到兩家客戶。

10. Q：滿產能時的營收貢獻預估？何時能回收資本支出？   
A：雷射封裝商業模式以加工費為主，營收絕對金額佔比看似較小，但扣除材料費後，純
加工毛利率遠高於傳統觸控顯示業務。董事會對資本支出有嚴格的財務紀律，原則上必須
在少數幾年內完成回收。

11. Q：到 2H27可能 300mW以上的雷射市場變成熟，公司屆時產 能到位可以拿下多少市
佔? 
A：公司有接觸多位 high power 的客戶，每個客戶需求都很可觀，即便現在因為選舉政治
議題等潛在變數可能改變 AI的需求，但以整個產業來看，公司建置的產能僅占需求面的
一部分比例，仍有很大的增加產能的空間。

12. Q：與富采的 Micro LED及光通訊發展有何競爭或關聯？   
A：富采沒有做到封裝， GIS做封裝，雙方可能為上下游關係。富采 Micro LED偏向 TV巨
量轉移應用， GIS則聚焦 AR投影機光機應用，兩者 Micro LED大小差異很大。公司也與
客戶在探討 Micro LED或 Micro VCSEL 在光通訊領域的潛力，均處於樣品開發階段。另外
公司今年 4Q26或 1Q27 AR projector 可能會有小量出貨。

13. Q：光通訊相關的研發專案進度？以及信賴性測試（如 5000小時）的狀況？ FAU會朝
模組發展嗎？   
A：不便針對單一客戶及具體的信賴性測試時數評論。關於 FAU，初期聚焦於光學元件（如
Fiber holder、波導、 V型槽、超透鏡） 。公司已具備Metalens自行設計及製造的能力，因
其製程與目前開發的 AR眼鏡波導製程十分相近。

WESTPAC ECONOMICS  
SEPTEMBER CPI PREVIEW
Setting the pace for the run into year end
19 October 2026
Key points
• September CPI to rise 0.62%mth 
with the annual pace lifting to 4.1%yr 
from 4.0%yr. The seasonally adjusted 
CPI lifts 0.5%mth, a step down from 
the 0.7% increase in August.  
• September Trimmed Mean (TM) is 
set to lift 0.26%mth holding the 
annual pace steady at 3.6%yr. 
• As we noted in our review of the 
August CPI, our September quarter 
CPI estimate is unchanged at 1.3%qtr 
while the TM remained 0.9%qtr.  
• Auto fuel surged ahead of our 
expectations so along with signs of 
increase pass-through we lifted our 
December quarter CPI to 1.3%qtr/3.9%yr 
and the TM estimate to 0.9%qtr/3.5%yr. 
• This is a 0.8ppt increase to the CPI 
estimate, and a 0.2ppt increase to the 
TM estimate, highlighting inflation 
momentum meaningfully above 
the RBA’s current expectations.

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
BULLETIN
August inflation came in soft...
Inflation moderated in August following July’s strong 
outcome. The CPI rose 0.4% in the month, in line with our 
expectation but slightly below market consensus of 0.5%, 
taking the annual rate to 4.0%. On a seasonally adjusted 
basis, the CPI increased 0.7%mth, underscoring the impact of 
seasonal discounting in August and the start of the financial 
year repricing in July.
More closely watched are the measures of underlying inflation 
including the Trimmed Mean (TM) and market services. 
The TM rose 0.2% in August, in line with our expectations 
but again softer than the market, leaving the annual pace 
unchanged at 3.6% for a third consecutive month. Meanwhile, 
market services excluding volatiles & holiday travel increased 
0.2%mth/4.1%yr. Notable strength (three-month annualised, 
seasonally adjusted) was seen in sports participation (10.9%), 
clothing repair (7.0%), restaurant meals (6.8%) and other 
household services (5.2%). For full analysis of the August CPI, 
see “August CPI – Came in on our expectations ”.
The expenditure-weighted share of the CPI basket 
experiencing positive price shocks eased to 11% in August 
from 15% in July, while the share experiencing negative price 
shocks declined from 17% to just below 13%. The majority of 
the positive shocks were concentrated in consumer durables 
On a three-month moving average basis, the net momentum 
share (positive minus negative) rose to –4.5% from –7.7% 
in July and a trough of –15.8% in May, suggesting the 
disinflationary impulse may be nearing exhaustion. 
... with September due for a moderate pick-up
Looking ahead to September, early indicators painted a 
mixed picture. The NAB business survey showed retail price 
growth slowing to 0.9%qtr from 1.1%qtr in August, with 
labour cost growth easing to 1.7%qtr from 1.8%qtr, while 
purchase cost growth edged up to 2.4%qtr from 2.3%qtr. 
Nevertheless, all measures remain well above their long-run 
averages while the gap between purchase costs and final 
prices continues to widen, indicating businesses are facing 
margin compression (see here for further detail). Meanwhile, 
consumers’ year-ahead inflation expectations held steady 
at 4.9% in September (easing slightly at 2dp) but surged to 
5.3% in October likely reflecting higher fuel prices, with retail 
petrol and diesel averaging $2.20/l (+8.2%mth) and $2.68/l 
(+7.7%mth). We do believe the auto fuel prices are likely to see 
a near term peak in October.  Meanwhile, global shipping costs 
have also surged recently amid renewed Middle East tensions.
Sep quarter unrevised but Dec upgraded significantly
Justin Smirk, Senior Economist
Neha Sharma, Economist

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
BULLETIN
The September CPI is expected to rise 0.6% (0.62% to 2dp)
with the annual pace lifting to 4.1%yr. Our seasonally adjusted
estimate increases 0.5% (0.48% to 2dp). For the monthly
TM, we expect a 0.3% (0.26% to 2dp) lift, holding the annual

pace steady at 3.6%. On a 3m/3m basis, the TM is tracking
a 1.0% gain in September (noting that while this measure is
conceptually different to the quarterly TM it still serves as a
useful guide). For market services excluding volatiles & holiday
travel we have pencilled in another 0.2% rise, slowing the
annual pace slightly to 4.0% (a nine-month low).
The largest contributors are recreation & culture (+0.27ppt),
driven wholly by holiday travel & accommodation. Transport

(+0.23ppt) makes a solid contribution, with gains in fuel
(+0.26ppt) partly offset by motor vehicles (–0.02ppt). Housing
also contributes (+0.13ppt), with rents and new dwellings
each adding 0.02ppt while property rates add 0.08ppt.
The key detractors in the month are food & non-alcoholic
beverages (–0.08ppt) and garments (–0.04ppt).
Key details for the month of September
Food & non-alcoholic beverages are expected to fall
0.5%mth, easing the annual pace to 2.8%yr. The weakness is

concentrated in a 3.7%mth decline in fruit & vegetables, while
non-alcoholic beverages are also expected to fall 1.5%mth.
Meals out & takeaway is forecast to rise 0.1%mth, easing the
annual rate to 3.9%.
Housing is expected to rise 0.6% in September, slowing the
annual pace to 5.5%yr. Component-wise:
• Rents are expected to rise 0.3%mth and 3.7%yr. CPI rent
inflation is nearing its peak, consistent with the moderation
in asking rent growth. We expect rent inflation to ease to

3.6%yr by end-2026 and 2.5%yr by end-2027.
• New dwelling prices, which capture only the building
component of construction costs, are expected to rise
0.2%mth and 4.9%yr. Ongoing weakness in established
housing markets would be increasing builders’ difficulty in
passing through higher costs to new builds. As a result, we
expect new dwelling inflation to moderate to 4.5%yr by
end-2026 and 2.1%yr by end-2027.
Transport inflation is expected to rise 2.0%mth in September,

lifting the annual pace to 7.7%yr reflecting an 8.3%mth
increase in fuel prices following higher oil prices through the
month. Motor vehicle prices and spare parts are expected to
fall 0.7%mth and 1.3%mth, respectively. In NSW, a one-year
vehicle registration discount between $80-100 came into
effect from 1 September, which is likely to weigh on the other
services in respect of motor vehicles component.
Recreation & culture is expected to rise 2.1%mth driven by a

4.4%mth increase in holiday travel, partly offset by a 0.4%
decline in audio, visual & computing equipment & services and
a 1.3% fall in sporting services.

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
Risks to September baseline
We view the risks around the September CPI outcome as broadly 
balanced. Waning spending momentum outside of fuel may make 
it difficult for businesses to pass through higher costs in the near 
term. However, some of these pricing pressures may be deferred 
rather than forgone. The October ban on card surcharging may 
prompt a greater-than-usual degree of price resetting, allowing 
businesses to recover some of the margin pressures that have built 
up in recent months.
Risks to housing are mixed. Trade materials suppliers continued 
to announce above-average price increases through September 
(around 8%). While soft conditions in the established housing 
market may limit the extent to which these costs are passed 
through to new dwelling prices, they nevertheless present an 
upside risk to housing-related inflation, particularly maintenance 
& repair and insurance costs. Growth in advertised rents also 
continues to ease but these new leases take some time to flow 
through to average rents paid measured in the CPI. 
A kick up in October ...
We have a solid preliminary estimate for the October CPI of 
0.6%mth lifting the annual pace to 4.8%yr. It is true that the key 
driver is an 8.9% increase in auto fuel but we estimate the TM to 
lift 0.4%mth/3.7%yr highlighting broader inflationary pressures. 
We expect that the removal of credit charge surcharges and 
resulting increase in retail prices, at most, will contribute 0.1ppt 
but only just so see it as more of a rounding error. Nevertheless, 
these changes come at a time of a significant upstream price 
pressure, particularly for SMEs, so there is some risk of broader 
price adjustments. As such, we expect marginally larger than 
usual price adjustments in October particularly in hospitality 
and household services. Being the first month of the quarter the 
October lift sets a higher base for the quarterly average estimates 
resulting in an upgrade to our December quarter estimate.
We have included a table with a monthly profile for the CPI, 
seasonally adjusted CPI and the TM out to June 2027. At 
June 2027 we have an annual pace of 3.7%yr for the CPI and 
seasonally adjusted CPI and 3.3%yr for the TM. There is also a 
table breaking down the monthly components of the CPI through 
the December quarter. 
... highlights stronger inflationary pressure ...
Our September quarter CPI estimate is unrevised at 1.3%qtr 
with the annual pace steady at 3.9%yr. At the margin there 
was a 0.04ppt increase due to a lift in auto fuel but this is 
1.29%qtr at two decimal places so still rounds to 1.3%. 
The seasonally adjusted CPI gains 1.1% in the September 
quarter highlighting that part of the increase is due to usual 
seasonality. Our TM estimate for Q3 has a small downward 
revision of 0.03ppt to 0.88% at two decimal place so our 
published estimate is also unrevised at 0.9%qtr. 
... resulting in an upward revision to Q4 2026
Westpac’s December quarter CPI estimate has been boosted 
from a 0.5%qtr to 1.3%qtr. Driving most of this change from 
our previous estimate is the 15.3% increase in autofuel, it

was 1.9%, contributing 0.55ppt to the 0.8ppt increase in the 
quarterly estimate. Outside of a 0.09ppt contribution to the 
revision from holiday travel & accommodation (revised up from 
4.2% to 5.6%) all else was via small contributions for a large 
number of expenditure categories. This is why there is a 0.2ppt 
increase in our September quarter TM estimate from 0.7%qtr 
to 0.9%qtr.
A disinflationary impulse kicks in for 2027
We now expect auto fuel prices to peak in October (240 cpl 
for petrol, 284 cpl for diesel) and then to trend lower through 
2027 (184 cpl for petrol and 194 cpl for diesel by December 
2027) leading to a 13.6% falling in auto fuel in the first half 
of 2027 subtracting 0.46ppt off the CPI over the same period 
of time. For a detailed description on how we constructed the 
auto fuels profile please see: “ Persistent energy pressures 
have further to run ” 
The first half of 2027 will also see an almost 5% decline in 
holiday travel & accommodation which will subtract a further 
0.29ppt off the CPI. However, outside of that any broader 
moderation in inflation is expected to be more muted hence 
we have the TM printing 0.7%qtr for each quarter in 2027.  
Critical to this moderation in core inflation is our expect step 
down in dwelling price inflation on the back of falling estab -
lished home prices. From a 1.1%qtr increase in Q3 2026 we 
see dwelling price increasing by 0.7% in the Q4 2026, and 
then 0.6% in both Q1 and Q2 2027 before dropping to 0.5%qtr 
in Q3 and Q4 2027. This will see dwelling price inflation in the 
CPI drop from 5.4%yr at June 2026 to 3.0%yr by June 2027 
then down to 2.2%yr by December 2027. 
At least for the near term a key uncertainty remains the extent 
to which businesses are able to pass through ongoing labour 
and non-labour cost pressures and how this unwinds through 
2027 as energy prices ease and the domestic economy slows 
under the weight of higher interest rates and falling estab -
lished home prices. There is a risk that firms face greater 
resistance to price increases if discretionary household spend -
ing softens by more than expected. That said, these headwinds 
may be at least partially offset by the stronger starting point 
for household incomes, which has proven more resilient than 
previously anticipated, and by higher energy prices due to the 
ongoing conflict in the Middle East.
That said, there is also the risk that the disinflationary pulse by 
the second half of 2027 could be greater than what we antici-

and thus a more significant moderation in price setting behav -
iour. It is also possible that our auto fuel prices fall by more 
than we anticipate. For while we have spot crude prices to fall 
to about pre-crisis levels by end 2027, a combination of a slow 
recovery in refining capacity, constrained return of physical 
supplies and higher shipping cost keep Australian fuel prices 
above their pre-conflict level. Should all these factors improve 
fast than we expect, and auto fuel prices return to pre-conflict 
level if not lower, then headline inflation could dip below 2%yr 
and the TM would be closer to 2.5%yr (the mid point if the 
RBA’s inflation target), by end 2027.

投資與評價 
1. 群聯 3Q26受惠 AI Ecosystem及 Enterprise SSD需求強勁，營運創歷
史新高；預期 4Q26隨 AI相關產品佔比提升，營收成長動能延續。 
2. 群聯 26 年受惠 AI 資料中心需求擴張、NAND 價格上漲與 Enterprise 
SSD放量，加上低成本庫存優勢及AI Ecosystem佔比提升，EPS 455.55
元，營運創歷史新高；27年 AI推論及 KV Cache Offload 帶動高容量
SSD需求，PCIe Gen6及 aiDAPTIV接棒成長，EPS 380.70元。 
3. 目前群聯交易在 1,500元(3.9X 27(F) PER)，位於近四年本益比參考區間
3~20X下緣位置，考量預期 27年 AI system module 需求強勁，營收
佔比持續提高，維持買進投資建議，但因27年毛利率可能隨低成本庫存
效益消退而降低，獲利成長動能趨緩，故本益比由區間下緣之 8倍調低
至接近區間下緣之 5倍，目標價 1,900.0 元(5 x 修正後 FY27E EPS)。 
重點更新 
群聯 3Q26企業級 SSD放量帶動營運創新高，AI布局成果逐步顯現 
群聯 3Q26受惠 AI基礎建設持續擴張，企業級 SSD、AI伺服器 Boot Drive
及 aiDAPTIV等高附加價值產品需求強勁，帶動AI生態系統營收 QoQ+88%，
營收佔比提升至 57%。儘管傳統PC、手機及工業市場需求疲弱，Embedded 
ODM及 Industrial分別季減-7%及 30%，但控制晶片出貨量仍逆勢成長。
3Q26營收 860.76億元(+26.8% QoQ)，符合預期。受 NAND Flash採購成
本上升及產品售價調整時間差影響，部分抵銷低成本庫存及產品組合優化效
益，毛利率 59.7%(-5.6ppts QoQ)，營業利益 328.98億元(+24.8% QoQ)，
稅後淨利 305.48億元(+16.5% QoQ)，EPS 138.00元，營運創歷史新高。  
預期 EnterpriseSSD與 Controller成長延續推升群聯 3Q26營運再創新高  
預期群聯 4Q26受惠北美 CSP持續擴張 AI基礎設施，企業級 SSD、高容量
QLC SSD及 Boot Drive需求強勁，隨 Agentic AI及 KV Cache Offload 應
用逐步導入，進一步推升 AI伺服器 SSD容量需求，10月 AI生態系統營收
佔比再提升至 65%。因下調NAND價格漲幅，小幅下修4Q26營收預估 2.1%
至 972.08億元(+12.9% QoQ)。惟隨低成本庫存逐步消化，NAND採購成本
上升及產品售價調整時間差壓抑獲利，調降毛利率預估 1.6 個百分點至
56.2%(-3.5ppts QoQ)，營業利益下修至 325.75億元(-1.0% QoQ)，稅後淨
利下修至 288.18億元(-5.7% QoQ)，EPS 130.18元，營收續創歷史新高。

### Page 2

訪談內容 
群聯 3Q26企業級 SSD放量帶動營運創新高，AI布局成果逐步顯現 
群聯 3Q26受惠 AI基礎建設持續擴張，企業級SSD、AI伺服器 Boot Drive、AI PC及 aiDAPTIV
等高附加價值產品需求強勁，帶動 AI生態系統相關營收 QoQ+88%，營收佔比提升至 57%，
其中 Tier-1 CSP、Rack系統及 OEM/ODM伺服器客戶持續導入群聯儲存解決方案，推升企業
級 SSD及客製化產品出貨。儘管傳統 PC、智慧型手機及工業市場受到 NAND Flash價格上漲
與零組件缺料影響，Embedded ODM 及 Industrial業務分別季減 7%及 30%，消費性市場需
求亦明顯下滑，但控制晶片出貨量仍逆勢成長，反映公司在 Client SSD 及 UFS控制晶片市場
的市佔率持續提升；截至3Q26，群聯亦已取得三家NAND原廠共 6項 PCIe Gen5 TLC及 QLC 
Client SSD 控制晶片設計專案，為後續出貨奠定基礎，帶動 3Q26營收 860.76億元(+26.8% 
QoQ)，符合預期。 
雖然 AI與企業級高附加價值產品比重增加，以及先前建立的 NAND庫存成本優勢有助維持獲
利，但隨 NAND Flash採購價格持續上漲，新進料成本逐步反映於銷貨成本，加上產品售價調
整存在時間差，部分抵銷產品組合優化效益，另存貨跌價損失影響毛利率約0.34個百分點，毛
利率 59.7%(-5.6ppts QoQ)，略低於預期。在 PCIe Gen5/Gen6 控制晶片、UFS 5.0、企業級
SSD及 aiDAPTIV等新產品持續開發下，先進製程光罩、工程驗證及AI軟體研發支出增加，加
上公司持續擴充研發人員及相關獎酬，研發費用達152.88億元，營業費用184.89億元(+2.9% 
QoQ)，營業利益 328.98億元(+24.8% QoQ )，權益法認列投資收益 28.85億元推動業外利益
超出預期，稅後淨利 305.48億元(+16.5% QoQ)，EPS 138.00元，營運創歷史新高。

### Page 3

預期群聯 4Q26 EnterpriseSSD與 Controller成長延續，營收再創新高 
預期群聯 4Q26受惠北美 CSP持續擴張 AI推論基礎設施，企業級 SSD、高容量 QLC SSD 及
AI伺服器 Boot Drive需求持續增加，隨 Agentic AI及 KV Cache Offload應用逐步導入，SSD
除傳統資料儲存外，亦開始承擔AI推論過程中 KV Cache保存及重用功能，進一步提高單台AI
伺服器的 SSD容量需求；10月 AI生態系統營收佔比進一步提升至 65%。NAND原廠產能優
先配置高附加價值產品，TLC及 QLC供應持續緊俏，我們預估 4Q26 NAND Flash合約價季增
高個位數百分比，但企業級 SSD合約價漲幅可達 15~20%，有利群聯持續調整產品售價；公司
亦持續透過與 NAND原廠合作確保供貨，目前存貨達 1,117.74億元，其中企業級應用佔50%，
超過 90%配置於高附加價值市場，支撐後續訂單交付。 
因下條 4Q26 NAND 價格漲幅，故小幅下調 4Q26 營收預估 2.1%至 972.08 億元(+12.9% 
QoQ)，隨 NAND Flash成本持續上漲，前期低成本庫存逐步消化，新採購成本開始增加銷貨成
本，且部分客戶產品售價調整存在時間差，加上消費性PC、智慧型手機及工業應用受高價零組
件影響，產品組合持續朝企業級及AI應用調整，調低毛利率預估 1.6個百分點至 56.2%(-3.5ppts 
QoQ)。調降營業利益預估至 325.75 億元(-1.0% QoQ )，下修稅後淨利至 288.18 億元(-5.7% 
QoQ)，EPS 130.18元，營收仍續創歷史新高。

### Page 4

Phison 3.0轉型效益發酵，AI高附加價值業務推升群聯營運創歷史新高 
群聯 26年受惠全球 AI基礎建設投資持續增加，NAND Flash供需結構轉向企業級及 AI應用，
帶動企業級 SSD、高容量 TLC/QLC儲存及 AI伺服器 Boot Drive 需求快速成長，AI生態系統
相關營收於已佔整體近 65%，成為主要營運成長動能。控制晶片方面，儘管26年傳統 PC及智
慧型手機需求疲弱，且Embedded ODM及 Industrial市場受到 NAND Flash漲價與零組件缺
料影響，但群聯控制晶片出貨量仍逆勢成長，反映市佔率持續提升；此外，目前已取得三家 NAND
原廠共 6項 PCIe Gen5 TLC及 QLC Client SSD控制晶片設計專案，並持續擴大 UFS 4.1控制
晶片出貨，協助 NAND合作夥伴將 QLC導入 UFS 4.1 量產，同時取得多項 UFS 5.0 控制晶片
Design Win，進一步鞏固 PC及手機儲存控制晶片市場競爭力。 
企業級應用方面，群聯持續擴大與 Tier-1 CSP、伺服器 OEM/ODM及 Rack系統客戶合作，取
得企業級 SSD、Boot Drive 及客製化儲存 Design Win，並透過 Pascari品牌擴大企業級儲存
產品布局。另一方面也持續推進 Phison 3.0 轉型，將業務由傳統控制晶片與 SSD模組延伸至
AI基礎設施及軟體服務，透過 Phison AI Data Platform 整合 GPU伺服器、Cache Server、
aiDAPTIV與企業級 SSD，推動金融、醫療、教育及政府等地端 AI應用，目前已取得 17家金
融機構、85家中小企業、10家醫院、18所大專院校及 9個政府單位的導入機會，逐步建立軟
硬體整合商業模式。 
因下調 4Q26 NAND 價格，微幅下修 26年營收預估 0.9%至 2,921.40億元(+302.0% YoY)。
受惠年內 NAND Flash價格持續上漲，先前建立的低成本庫存優勢，以及企業級SSD、AI儲存
與控制晶片產品組合改善，推升整體營業毛利，惟隨下半年NAND採購成本上升，調降預毛利
率預估 0.6個百分點至 60.1%(+25.9ppts YoY)，因調低新產品相關研發費用預估，調高營業利
益預估至 1,066.84億元(+1,190.6% YoY)，微幅上調稅後淨利預估至 1,007.61億元(+1,052.7% 
YoY)，EPS 455.55元，營運創歷史新高。

預期群聯 27年高階儲存需求持續升溫，營收續創高峰 
預期 27年隨全球 AI應用逐步由模型訓練轉向大規模推論部署，AI伺服器、Agentic AI及企業
地端 AI應用持續擴張，對高容量企業級SSD及 KV Cache Offload的需求進一步提高，尤其在
LLM 推論過程中，透過 SSD保存及重用 KV Cache，可降低重複 Prefill運算需求，提高相同
GPU配置下的推論效率與同時服務使用者數量，進一步推升TLC及 QLC SSD容量與出貨需求。
群聯目前已布局相關 KV Cache Offload SSD，並持續開發 PCIe Gen6 TLC企業級 SSD，預計
隨新一代伺服器 CPU及 AI平台於 27年導入，逐步擴大高效能企業級SSD出貨；此外AI伺服
器 Boot Drive需求持續增加，搭配 PCIe Gen6 Retimer及客製化 AI儲存解決方案，可望提高
在 AI伺服器儲存及高速傳輸系統的參與程度。 
Client SSD方面，群聯先前取得的 6項 PCIe Gen5 TLC 及 QLC控制晶片 Design Win將逐步
進入後續產品導入及出貨階段，搭配 UFS 4.1 QLC持續放量與 UFS 5.0新專案推進，有助擴大
PC及手機儲存控制晶片市佔率；另外隨 Intel、AMD及 NVIDIA等業者持續推動 AI PC，群聯
將透過 aiDAPTIV 結合 SSD 及記憶體延伸技術，降低 AI PC 執行大型 AI 模型所需的高容量
DRAM配置成本，預期 2G27 AI PC滲透率逐步提升，帶動相關 SSD及控制晶片需求。 
群聯持續與 NAND原廠協商 27年供貨，爭取滿足70%以上的目標需求量，且3Q26底存貨中
超過 90%配置於高附加價值市場，將有助於維持企業級及 AI產品交付能力。 
在企業級 SSD、AI伺服器儲存、Client SSD控制晶片及地端 AI解決方案持續擴張下，上調 27
年營收預估 0.2%至 4,186.33億元(+43.3% YoY)。儘管企業級 SSD及 AI軟硬體整合業務持續
增加，但隨26年累積的低成本 NAND庫存逐步消化，27年銷貨成本將更多反映前期高價採購
的 NAND，加上消費性市場對價格上漲的接受度有限，以及高容量 SSD出貨增加帶動 NAND
材料成本佔比提高，小幅下調毛利率預估 1.3個百分點至 46.3%(-13.8ppts YoY)。

### Page 5

因下調新產品開發、軟體平台建置及研發人力擴編之營業費用預估，上調營業利益預估 5.1%至
959.35億元(-10.1% YoY)，調升稅後淨利預估至 842.75億元(-16.4% YoY)，EPS 380.70元，
營收續創歷史新高。

AI驅動儲存需求持續擴張，群聯營運邁向成長高峰 
隨 AI推論需求持續擴張，企業級 SSD、Boot Drive 及 KV Cache Offload 應用將帶動高容量
NAND需求成長，群聯憑藉控制晶片設計優勢、企業級SSD產品布局及充足庫存，有望持續擴
大 AI儲存市場市佔率。此外，隨 PCIe Gen6、UFS 5.0及 AI PC逐步導入，搭配 aiDAPTIV與
AI Data Platform 商業化，將推動公司由傳統儲存供應商轉型為 AI 軟硬體整合解決方案提供
者。儘管NAND採購成本上升及研發費用增加將壓抑短期獲利表現，惟高附加價值產品比重提
升及 AI生態系統持續擴張，仍將支撐中長期營運成長。 
目前群聯股價交易在 1,500元(3.9X 27(F) PER)，位於近四年本益比參考區間3~20X下緣位置，
考量預期 27年 NAND價格仍將居高不下，營收有望持續創高，AI system module需求強勁，
營收佔比持續提高，維持買進投資建議，但因 27年毛利率可能隨低成本庫存效益消退而降低，
獲利成長動能趨緩，故本益比由區間下緣之 8倍調低至接近區間下緣之 5倍，目標價 1,900.0 
元(5 x 修正後 FY27E EPS)。

### Page 6

公司簡介 
群聯電子成立於 2000年，為台灣 NAND flash控制晶片廠商，主要業務為 NAND flash控制
晶片及模組產品的研發設計製造及銷售，提供客戶控制晶片與 NAND Flash模組的一站式整合
儲存解決方案，強化與同業之間的產品差異性。公司產品項目包含內嵌式 SSD、UFS與 eMMC
與外接式 SD Card、USB等控制晶片與模組，並且控制晶片設計主要投片於台積電與聯電；營
運模式上游供給主要為 NAND原料供應商 KIOXIA、Micron等，下游客戶主要為各大伺服器系
統廠/PC OEM等，國內外同業包括記憶體控制晶片廠慧榮科技(SIMO)、NAND模組廠 Kingston、
威剛等。

表三、群聯產品應用說明 
資料來源:群聯、國泰證期研究部彙整

### Page 7

群聯 ESG政策 
群聯主要為 NAND Flash控制 IC及周邊系統產品的研發設計製造及銷售，產業類別歸類在半
導體行業中，依據專門為投資人設計的永續揭露框架 SASB準則，半導體行業關注重點為下列
幾項：溫室氣體排放、能源管理、水和廢水管理、廢棄物和危險材料管理、員工健康與安全、
員工多元化和包容性、產品設計與生命週期管理、材料採購與效率。由於群聯主要從事設計及
研發，研究部將篇幅著重在員工相關議題，後續則對溫室氣體排放、能源管理議題描述。

1. 員工多元化和包容性 
2023年國內正職員工中新進人數共計 374人，新進員工比例為 9.9%，其中男性 279人，女
性 95人；國內正職員工中離職人數共計 229人，離職員工比例為 6.1%，其中男性 151人，
女性 78人，以性別結構區分，本公司女性董事比例為 12.5%，若排除 R&D部門，女性員工之
比例佔 55%；以國籍劃分，台灣員工佔比 96.6%最高，第二高為馬來西亞籍，第三高及第四高
為越南及菲律賓，身心障礙員工僱用占比約 0.82%，持續落實績效評核制度，提升員工福利及
滿足員工職涯發展與優質人才留任率，並且針對不同群體擬訂不同之職場多元化方案，讓不同
背景的員工，都能在職場上受到接納與支持。

圖二、女性工作者、年齡比例統計 
資料來源：群聯、國泰證期研究部彙整

2. 員工健康與安全 
2023年員工無發生職業傷害及工傷死亡事件，員工總傷害事故為 22件，均為通勤導致之交通
事故。此外，2023年廠內承攬商作業申請共計有 345件，總工時約為 124萬小時，承攬商發
生兩件工傷事故，職業傷害比率為 1.61，後續皆已完成事故調查並採取矯正預防措施，以防止
同類型工傷事故再發生，因此本年度無承攬商發生死亡事件，在失能傷害頻率、嚴重率與傷害
比率均為 0%，公司持續以廠內重大職災件數維持 0件為本公司職業安全衛生的績效目標。

### Page 8

圖三、工傷分析統計 
資料來源:群聯，國泰證期研究部彙整

1. 溫室氣體排放： 
2022年類別一及類別二共排放 15,560.63公噸二氧化碳當量。相較 2021年，2022年因外購
電力變多，總排放量增加 4,185.37公噸；2022年因市場調整需求導致營收減少，排放密集度
上升到 0.258公噸二氧化碳當量/百萬營收，比 2021年增加 41.76%。公司每年推行各項節能
專案並購買可再生能源，持續向淨零的目標邁進，但過程中會受再生能源供給量及政府碳管理
政策等原因影響。 
圖四、 2019~2022溫室氣體排放量 
資料來源:群聯，國泰證期研究部彙整

2. 能源管理： 
群聯自 2021年開始購買再生能源(風電)，為積極響應減排，將原本計畫的可再生能源購買量加
倍，除維持初期以購買總用電比例 1%的目標，至 2023 年提升至 3%，至 2025 年再增加到
10%，至 2030年達到 20%，以穩健腳步執行能源轉型，降低營運過程中溫室氣體的排放量。

產業聚焦降息前的美國汽車產業投資布局
2026/10/30
• 美國傳統車商迎來皮卡改款
週期
• 高油價使油電、電動車需求
提升
• 自駕車、軟體營收增加帶動
車商獲利
美國傳統車商迎來皮卡改款週期，獲利表現有望提升
美國傳統車廠近年轉向生產更多高價卡車和SUV，因部分SUV和皮卡的調整後息稅前利潤率高達20%、超
過其他車型，也使美國新車平均交易價格在9月超過5萬美元。福特與通用汽車即將在2026年年底至2027
年迎來全尺寸皮卡改款與更新計畫，由於在推出新款或新一代卡車後，通常會有2~3年較佳的銷售期，預
估福特和通用汽車在2027年及之後的獲利有機會持續提升。
美國政策轉為支持燃油車發展，但高油價使油電、電動車需求提升

儘管美國政策轉向支持燃油車發展 ，但自美伊戰爭後油價居高不下 ，使油電混合車3Q26在美國的市佔率
暴衝至16%，包括豐田 、現代等品牌在美國的市占率持續上升 。美國3Q26電動車銷量約佔新車總銷量
6.5%，高於2Q26的5.8%，顯示電動車在美國市場正重新站穩腳步 。由於油價上升使全球對電動車需求
增加，特斯拉2Q26、3Q26交車量也優於市場預期。
自駕車接受度提升，軟體營收增加帶動車商獲利
美國消費者對自動駕駛的認識與接受度正持續提升，而各車商正積極擴大自駕與軟體訂閱服務布局。通用
汽車預估其OnStar和Super Cruise自駕輔助營收在2026年將突破30億美元，福特汽車的軟體付費訂閱用
戶已達160萬戶。3Q26特斯拉FSD活躍用戶數已達174萬、年增67%，且北美新車FSD採用率超過75%。
相較傳統車商低於10%的硬體銷售利潤率，軟體毛利率高達50~70%，且訂閱制使車商部分營收轉為持續
性收入，將帶動其估值表現。

美國9月份輕型車年化銷售量約為
1,600萬輛，低於市場預估共識的
1,630萬輛 。儘管9月初的勞動節
促銷活動使購車回饋及零售銷售
增加，但同時銷售量年減 4%也顯
示市場需求正面臨更大挑戰。
今年上半年儘管面臨高利率 、高
車價等負面影響 ，但高收入族群
支撐使美國車市仍具韌性 。然而
隨著利率上升 、對消費者可負擔
性的懷疑日益增加 ，市場目前正
逐步將2026年美國輕型車銷售量
預估由 1,610萬輛下修至 1,560至
1,600萬輛區間。
聯準會在 9月升息一碼 ，使美國
目標利率區間上升至 3.75~4%，
且目前 市場普遍預期聯準會在今
年12月及2027年上半年將再各升
息一碼 。美國約有 75~85%新車
購買使用貸款或融資 ，利率持續
上升將直接推高車貸利率 ，進而
增加消費者的購車成本與負債壓
力，且利率上升也會造成核貸門
檻轉趨嚴格 ，使消費者對新車的

需求下降。
未來若油價 、通膨下降 ，市場重
新步入降息循環後有望使美國車
市需求重新回升。資料來源：Cox Automotive，2026/9/24，中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/10/22

福特、通用汽車全尺寸皮卡在改款當年及之後
兩年銷售表現優於同業
資料來源：Goldman Sachs，2026/10/13，中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/10/22
車商將迎來皮卡改款週期
回饋優惠在交易價中佔比美國新車交易價格美國新車平均交易價格( ATP )在
9月超過5萬美元，車價上漲在每

年下半年為常見現象 ，以因應車
款更新而重新定價 。然而 ，9月
ATP年增率低於1%遠低於典型的
9月增幅2~4%，顯示利率上升後
平價車款銷售相對強勁。
儘管美國新車售價成長幅度趨緩 ，
但其上升趨勢仍使美國傳統車商
利潤率逐步改善 。通用、福特汽
車等傳統車廠近年 轉向生產更多
卡車和 SUV，因部分 SUV和皮卡
的調整後息稅前利潤率超過 20%，
使得車廠利潤率能維持一定水準 。
(美元)
福特與通用汽車即將在 2026年年
底至2027年迎來全尺寸皮卡改款
與 更 新 計 畫， 福 特 旗 艦 皮 卡F-
150將換上全新3.0升V6引擎，通
用汽車則將對 GMC Sierra 及
Chevrolet Silverado全尺寸皮卡
進行大改款。
鑑於福特與通用汽車在皮卡銷售
中取得的較高利潤 ，皮卡市場對
獲利的重要性可想而知 。由於原
廠在推出新款或新一代卡車後 ，
通常會有 2~3年較佳的銷售期 ，
預估福特和通用汽車在 2027年及
之後的獲利有機會持續提升。

4
高油價使美國電動車銷售逐步回溫
資料來源：Cox Automotive，2026/10/12，中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/10/28
需求使油電、電動車並存
康寧儘管政策轉向支持燃油車發展 ，
但自美伊戰爭後油價居高不下 ，
使油電混合車3Q26在美國的市佔
率暴衝至 16%，包括豐田 、現代
等品牌在美國的市占率持續上升 。

而儘管美國3Q26電動車銷量較去
年同期下降 (3Q25為電動車補助
期限 ，基期較高 )，但仍較 2Q26
成長近8%，約佔美國新車總銷量
的6.5%，高於2Q26的5.8%，顯
示電動車在美國市場正重新站穩
腳步。以市場反應來看 ，美國車
市正從先前單一電動化路線 ，轉
向燃油 、油電、電動車並存局面 。
美國國會在 2025年9/30 正式取
消購買或租賃新電動車的 7,500
美元稅收抵免 ，造成美國電動車
銷售在 4Q25開始大幅下滑 。另
外，川普政府在今年 9月底正式
宣布放寬美國企業燃油效率標準 ，
將未來新車的燃油效率年均提升
目標下調 ，使部分車商開始降低
電動車生產以減少虧損。
傳統車企如福特 、通用汽車的電
動車部門 EBIT利潤率可能介於 -
10~-30%以下，燃油效率標準放
寬使其生產方向轉回燃油車發展 ，
也有助於改善整體獲利結構。

各車商正積極擴大自駕與軟體訂閱
服務布局，並將其視為未來重要獲

OnStar和Super Cruise自駕輔助營

3Q26特斯拉 FSD活躍用戶數已達
174 萬 、 年增 67% ， 且 北 美 新 車
FSD採用率超過75%。
相較傳統車商低於 10%的硬體銷售
利潤率，軟體毛利率高達 50~70%，
且訂閱制使車商部分營收轉為持續

自駕車有較多了解的民眾比例由
40%增至58%，且近半數消費者相

另外，消費者雖然支持車輛自動化
發展，但仍傾向保有人工接管能力 ，
因此對 Level 4 的 興 趣 最 高， 對
Level 5的興趣反而下降。
調查也發現，多數曾搭乘Robotaxi
的使用者對自動駕駛的觀感明顯改
善，未來隨著服務覆蓋範圍持續擴
大，Robotaxi有望進一步提升大眾
對自駕技術的信心與接受度。 資料來源：Cox Automotive，2026/9/28，中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/10/29

市場投資展望
《臉書 Muse 點燃追趕行情，谷歌困於模型商品化
與旗艦空窗》
臉書代理式 AI 產品 Muse 迅速爆紅，推動股價單日
創 13 個月來最佳表現。市場開始將臉書從「AI 資
本支出大戶」重新定位為「具備自有 AI 爆款產品的
平台」。月底臉書乘勝進軍企業 AI 平台。然而，AI
代理競爭仍在初期 ，臉書雖挾分發優勢 ，但變現仍
待觀察。谷歌則在法務與搜尋基本面接連傳出利多 。
然而，OpenAI 與 Anthropic 接連大砍 API 價格導
致 AI 推理商品化疑慮，加上旗艦模型 Gemini 4 仍

遲遲未推出 ，谷歌股價因此持續承壓 。資本支出強
度攀升導致的 ROIC 走弱的核心敘事尚未見到反轉 ，
因此通訊服務仍維持中性看待。
• 9 月臉書推出面向消費端的 AI
代理 Muse，正面回應市場持
續關注的變現疑問。
• 臉書近期也成立企業 AI 平台，
將代理人技術延伸至企業端。
• 臉書成功回應市場對臉書有無
AI 應用產品的疑慮。但投入回
收成效仍待進一步驗證。
• 谷歌整合式廣告業務營運未受
結構性衝擊，搜尋有自 AI 競
爭者收復失土的跡象。
• 但谷歌在 AI 模型層仍面臨 AI
兩大壓力，不利市場信心。
1
資產 4Q26 看法 投資展望
通訊服務 中立 通訊服務主成分臉書近期推出消費端 AI 代理 Muse 與企業 AI 平台，
成功回應市場對於「臉書有沒有 AI 產品」的疑問，然後續 AI 投資變
現結果仍待逐步驗證。谷歌流失在廣告與搜尋業務迎來好消息，然在
AI 模型端面臨雙重逆風，不利市場信心。本行預期市場仍將聚焦檢視
資本支出效率與變現進度。靜候 10 月財報結果，維持中立評等。

資料來源：Koyfin、Capital IQ，2026/09/29；中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/09/29。
• 臉書 AI 投資變現路徑初具雛形，
消費端與企業端同步開展 P.2

變現面臨商品化與旗艦空窗雙重
逆風

零售客流量表
• 9 月臉書推出面向消費端的 AI

2
《臉書 AI 投資變現路徑初具雛形，消費端與企業端
同步開展》
9 月臉書推出面向消費端的 AI 代理 Muse，正面回
應市場持續關注的「臉書能否以自有 AI 產品在 40
億用戶基礎上變現」的疑問。Muse 是能代替用戶
執行比價、訂購、換約等具體任務的個人代理人。
上市後迅速登上美國 App Store 與 Google Play 下
載榜首，印證臉書既有社群入口與用戶關係在 AI 產

品分發上的結構性優勢。Muse 雖為臉書打開訂閱、
交易與導購分潤、廣告業務強化三條潛在變現路徑，
但其成敗仍取決於 Muse 能否延續上線初期的動能、
擴展至美國以外市場，並深入用戶日常使用情境。
同時，Muse 的代理能力正引發平台間的摩擦。亞
馬遜隨即封鎖其代理購物功能，拒絕第三方代理人
直接在其平台上替用戶下單，銀行、保險、旅遊等
仰賴消費者惰性賺取溢價的產業也可能採取類似的
防禦措施，交易分潤路徑能否成立，將取決於臉書
與既有平台的合作或議價能力。
緊接在 Muse 之後，臉書近期也宣布成立企業 AI 平
台，並延攬 MongoDB 執行長 CJ Desai 領導此一新
事業，以圖將代理人技術從消費端延伸至企業端。
臉書在企業市場的最大資產是全球大量中小企業已
用來與客戶溝通的 WhatsApp。在 WhatsApp 基礎
上衍生的自動處理詢價、下單、售後客服的企業 AI
代理應用具有相當的想像空間。不過，本行認為臉
書企業平台執行的不確定性明顯高於 Muse。主要
仍是考量到中小企業的數據品質、客戶信任與資安

風險與消費端截然不同。臉書過去缺乏企業銷售團
隊與服務紀錄，從零建立企業級通路需要時間與額
外投入，或將加重臉書的資本與費用負擔。
因此，本行認為 Muse 僅回應了市場對臉書 AI 投資
變現的第一個問題「臉書有沒有 AI 產品」。第二個
問題「投入何時能回收」，仍有待後續進展驗證。

資結果自行負責。未經本公司許可，本資料及訊息不得逕行抄錄、翻印或另作派發。本行以上市場資訊與分析，不涉及特定投資標的之建議。

• 谷歌整合式廣告業務營運未受
結構性衝擊，搜尋有自 AI 競
爭者收復失土的跡象。
• 但谷歌在 AI 模型層仍面臨 AI
推理商品化與旗艦模型空窗期
的壓力，不利市場信心。
3
《谷歌核心業務韌性無虞，但 AI 變現面臨商品化與
旗艦空窗雙重逆風》
9 月初聯邦法官駁回美國司法部拆分谷歌廣告技術
業務的訴求，改採行為矯正措施，意味谷歌廣告技
術堆疊得以保持完整，整合式廣告業務的營運模式
不受結構性衝擊，延續上半年 Android 應用商店案
和解落幕後法務風險持續收斂的趨勢。搜尋方面，
業界觀察顯示谷歌搜尋已在 ChatGPT 等 AI 競爭者
挑戰下收復部分使用量，AI Overviews 與 AI Mode

似乎有效留住用戶，緩解了 AI 聊天機器人侵蝕搜尋
入口的疑慮。
然而，谷歌在 AI 模型層面臨兩項壓力。第一是 AI
推理商品化。9 月下旬 OpenAI 推出 GPT-6 Sol 與
Luna 並將 API 價格砍半，Anthropic 亦發表 Opus
5.5，效能比肩前代頂級模型、成本卻降低 40%，並
同步上架三大雲端平台。前沿模型價格快速下探，
意味 Google Cloud 的 AI 推理營收單價與 Gemini
API 的定價空間在資本支出擴張之時同步受壓。價
格下行對使用量的影響動態將是市場在 10 月財報時
的觀察重點。
第二個壓力是旗艦模型的持續空窗。9 月下旬
DeepMind 新任主管暗示 Gemini 4 可望於年底前
推出，意味谷歌在 26Q4 之前將持續缺席前沿旗艦
競爭，但而同一期間 OpenAI 已推出 GPT-6、
Anthropic 推出 Opus 5.5。旗艦模型能力雖不必然
保證應用端的落地成效，但 Flash 等輕量模型與法
律、金融等垂直版本，多以旗艦模型為基礎蒸餾或

衍生，旗艦進度因此直接決定整體模型組合所能涵
蓋的應用場景廣度與深度。在上有閉源前沿模型、
下有低價開放權重模型持續滲透企業市場的夾擊下，
旗艦模型進度落後將持續壓抑市場對谷歌雲在企業
AI 應用的看法與信心。

Document 539: 凱基投顧_4915 致伸_余昀澄_20261105

Source PDF: all report pdf/凱基投顧_4915 致伸_余昀澄_20261105.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-11-05

### Page 1

十一月 05, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R  Powered by the EFA Platform 1

動態更新 電子零組件 ‧ 台灣

致伸 
(4915.TW /4915 TT)

毛利率低於預期 
重要訊息  
3Q26 EPS  0.83 元低於我們預估。管理層表示隨著多項新專案啟動 ，車用與
AIoT 業務將成為 2027 年主要營收成長動能。 
評論及分析  
3Q26 EPS 低於我們預期。3Q26 EPS 年減 46%、季減 34%至 0.83 元，較
凱基及市場共識預估低 35%，主因毛利率不如預期。毛利率季減 1.4ppts、
年減 3.6 ppts 至 13.4%，較凱基預估低 1.2 ppts，主因零組件價格上漲、人
民幣升值，以及產品組合不利，其中智慧生活營收 比重由 2Q26 的 25%提
升至 3Q26 的 27%。而因研發費用較高，營利率 2%亦較我們 預估低
1.5ppts。1-3Q26 EPS 為 3.38 元，年減 29%。 
4Q26 毛利率展望低於預期。管理層展望 4Q26 營收年增低雙位數，隱含季
減高個位數，符合我們預期。因公共安全 (警用相機)  客戶需求強勁，4Q26
車用/AIoT 營收 (3Q26 營收比重 32%) 可望年增 20-30%，隱含季增低個位
數。智慧生活營收 (比重 27%) 展望年增逾 20%，隱含季減低個位數。 資訊
產品營收 (比重 41%) 則可能小幅年減、季減高個位數。然而，管理層預期零
組件漲價將持續壓抑 4Q26 毛利率，抵銷產品組合優化 (車用/AIoT 營收比重
提高) 之挹注。公司展望費用率小幅年減，高於我們原估，我們因此下修獲利
率及 4Q26 EPS 預估至 0.19 元，2026 年 EPS 下修至 3.56 元，年減 38%。 
車用/AIoT 為 2027 年主要成長動能， 但 1H27 毛利率仍面臨壓力。 管理層
目標將目前消費性產品為主 的營收結構  (凱基預估 2026 比重 65%)，逐步轉
型為以 B2B 業務為主。因此，致伸將擴大車用/ AIoT 產品布局，包括公共安
全、安防及機器人市場。公司 展望高毛利率 的公共安全與安防未來三年營收
CAGR 維持雙位數，並預計 2027 年為日本客戶組裝服務型機器人， 美國機
器人市場則以供貨相機模組為主。 上述將帶動車用/ AIoT 營收比重由 2026
年 34%提升至 2027 年 38% (營收金額年增 20%)。然而，考量消費性需求
疲弱，我們認為 消費產品與客戶的價格協商進度將 較先前預期緩慢。 1H27
毛利率仍將維持低檔，最快需待 2H27 才有機會回升。因此，我們下修 毛利
率預估，並下修 2027 年 EPS 預估至 4.34 元，年增 22%。 
投資建議 
因獲利下修，目標價調降至 52元，基於 12x 2027F EPS，維持「持有」評等。 
投資風險  
消費需求較弱、新專案與客戶遞延。

圖 6：公司概況  圖 7：B2B相機需求強勁，帶動3Q26車用/AIoT營收比重季增
至 32%。 
致伸成立於 1984 年，專門製造消費型與企業電子產品。 2025
年營收 49%來自資訊產品、32%來自車電/工業物聯網、 19%
來自智慧生活 ；電腦設計製造代工  (ODM) 與電子製造服務  
(EMS)兩種服務模式並存。近年產品組合再從手機相機鏡頭模
組(CCM)拓展至商務、監視錄像與汽車產品，並藉收購迪芬尼
(Tymphany) 切入音響及耳機市場。

ESG 圖表定義 
項目 定義 說明 
能源使用 
直接與間接能源消耗總量(十億焦耳) 
- 公司運營範圍內消耗之能源總量 
- 能源使用總量 = 直接能源消耗總量 + 間接能源消耗量 
- 總能源使用量包括購買的能源、生產的能源 
- 就公用事業而言，輸電/電網損耗為其業務活動之一部分，被視為總能源消耗，數據不包括
為滿足能源使用而生產的電力（公用事業為出售而生產） 
- 就公用事業而言，用於能源生產的煤、天然氣或核能等原料不屬於「總能源使用」項下

購買之再生能源 
Primary再生能源購買總量(十億焦耳) 
- 公司各種來源之能源消耗量與購買的能源中屬於自然界可再生者（太陽能、風能、水能、
生物質能、地熱能）之量  
- 如無證據顯示再生能源由公司生產，則所報告的能源數據視為購買的再生能源

再生能源使用率 再生能源占總能源消耗量之比例  
CO2 約當排放量 
直接 CO2 與 CO2 約當排放量(公噸) 
- 公司擁有或控制的來源的直接排放量（範圍 1 排放量） 
- 相關氣體：二氧化碳 (CO2)、甲烷 (CH4)、一氧化二氮 (N2O)、氫氟碳化物 (HFCS)、全氟化
合物 (PFCS)、六氟化硫 (SF6)、三氟化氮 (NF3)

CO2 約當排放量營收比 
直接 CO2 與 CO2 約當排放量（公噸）銷售（百萬元）比 
- 公司擁有或控制的來源的直接排放量（範圍 1 排放量） 
- 相關氣體：二氧化碳 (CO2)、甲烷 (CH4)、一氧化二氮 (N2O)、氫氟碳化物 (HFCS)、全氟化
合物 (PFCS)、六氟化硫 (SF6)、三氟化氮 (NF3)

廢棄物總量 
廢棄物總量(公噸) 
- 總廢棄物 = 無害廢棄物 + 有害廢棄物 
- 僅考慮固體廢棄物，但如液體廢棄物以公噸為單位呈報，則會將其加入求得包含液體廢棄
物之總量 
- 對於採礦、石油與天然氣等行業，tailings、廢石、煤、飛灰等廢棄物亦考慮在內

廢棄物回收率 
公司呈報的廢棄物回收率 
- 廢棄物回收率 = 廢棄物回收量/總廢棄物*100 
- 廢棄物轉化為能源或經由廢棄物焚燒產生能源視為廢棄物回收 
- 經由堆肥回收的廢棄物視為回收之廢棄物

總取水量 
總取水量(立方公尺) 
- 由呈報組織直接或經水公司等中介機構從任何水源抽取的總水量 
- 水井、城鎮/公用事業/市政用水、河水、地表水等不同之水源均予以考慮

環保支出 
環保支出總金額 
- 所有用於環境保護，或防止、減少、控制環境因素、影響、危害的投資與支出，亦包括處
置、處理、衛生、清理支出

員工流動率 
員工流動率 
- 包括任何原因（自願或非自願）離開公司的員工，如辭職、退休、自然離職/死亡、醫療失
能、冗員、裁員、重組、解僱、裁減或定期合約到期 
- 員工流動率 =（離開之員工/平均員工人數）*100 
- 平均員工人數 =（本年末員工人數 + 去年末員工人數）/2 
- 本財年末員工數 =上個財年末員工數 + 新員工數 - 離開之員工數

女性經理 
女性經理百分比 
- 女性經理占公司經理的百分比 
- 如有不同階層之百分比，如最高階、高階、中階、初階，則會考慮中階女性經理之百分比 
- 女性經理百分比 = 女性經理人數/經理總數*100

女性員工 
女性員工百分比 
- 女性員工占公司員工總數的百分比 
- 女性員工百分比 = 女性人數/員工總數*100

教育訓練總時數 
所有員工教育訓練總時數 
- 僅考慮員工教育訓練時數 
- 包括一般員工所有類型的教育訓練（如健康與安全、環境、急難事件救援、技能與職業發
展） 
- 如果數據以天為單位，則乘以 8，係假設 1 天 =  8 小時工作

每位員工教育訓練時數 平均每年每位員工總教育訓練時數  
資料來源：Refinitiv；凱基

南华期货宏观周报
——中期选举尘埃落定，非农数据大超预期
翟帅男 （投资咨询证号：Z0013395）
联系邮件：zaisuainan@nawaa.com
投资咨询业务资格：证监许可【2011】1290号
2026年11月7日
..
主要观点
北京时间11月4日美国中期选举，民主党重掌参众两院。特朗普败因集中在生活成本、油价与伊朗战事。选举
结果出来后，对伊军事行动的政治约束上升，中东局势提前结束预期升温。但民主党席位没到三分之二，关
税与战争权限不足，国会的约束主要落在听证与拨款上，到本周结束仍没有正式停火文本，霍尔木兹附近船
只遇袭还在继续。民主党横扫也让AI与数据中心监管收紧的预期升温，压力先落到州长与地方审批环节。美
联储静默期结束后多位官员继续放鹰，目前12月加息仍是基准情景。周五晚上公布的10月非农大超预期，目
前美国就业市场整体强劲，经济对于加息不构成明显制约，也会进一步推升12月加息预期。接下来重点关注
下周的CPI数据和官员讲话。
资产端本周分化明显。中期选举落地，通胀与加息预期跟着松动，美元和美债收益率回落，A股和黄金明显反

弹；但随后AI严监管担忧升温带动美股科技板块大幅回调，A股同样承压。中债方面，短期仍偏强，但追高性
价比有限。原油主线仍是中东局势，关注后续美伊谈判进展。黄金短期底部企稳，但后续多空交织，预计年
内区间震荡为主。
风险提示：中东和谈破裂、局势再度升级，美国CPI超预期、美联储官员超预期放鹰
本期看点
1. 中期选举民主党大胜，如何影响地缘局势和AI发展？
2. A股和黄金为何在中期选举后同步反弹，反弹可持续吗？
3. 美国10月非农大超预期，美国就业真的很强吗？

第一章 一周市场感悟
本周海外主线围绕美国中期选举展开。美国中期选举民主党大获全胜，美国对伊军事行动与AI监管将面临政
治约束，中东从对抗转入谈判但和谈尚无实质进展。
1.1　美国中期选举，民主党大获全胜
北京时间11月4日美国中期选举，民主党重掌参众两院。截至北京时间6日5时，据美国有线电视新闻网预
测，在国会参议院，民主党控制54席，共和党控制44席，还有两席尚未确定；在国会众议院，民主党控制
253席，共和党控制155席。随着美国中期选举计票工作的持续推进，美国多家媒体预测，民主党在参众两院
的席位优势还可能进一步扩大。
本次选举，特朗普所在共和党失败原因普遍指向生活成本、油价与伊朗战事，特朗普接受结果但强调拥有完
全否决权，万斯公开把败因归于战事。选举结果对宏观的影响也很显然。国会拨款与军事授权的政治约束上
升，对伊军事行动提前收尾的概率被重新评估；民主党主导国会后，AI与数据中心监管收紧的预期同时升
温。
AI这条线的影响主要落在地方。数据中心的建设许可、并网审批与税收优惠由州长、州公用事业委员会和地

方规划部门掌握，联邦层面的全国性AI立法需要跨过总统否决，民主党席位没到三分之二，短期很难通过。
这次改选的36个州长里，得州、俄亥俄、佐治亚、内华达等数据中心集中州由共和党翻为民主党，几位新任
州长在竞选中提出暂停新建数据中心，或对审批附加成本、用工与环保条件。得州当选州长表示，可与马斯
克合作，但必须先停止数据中心建设。得州在建数据中心数量居全美前列，审批从等审查转为等立法之后，
新增项目的开工与订单节奏会被拖慢，新州长的表态也很大程度上会影响AI的监管预期。
1.2 美联储放鹰和非农大超预期
美联储静默期在11月2日结束，洛根、穆萨莱姆、巴尔金先后放鹰，洛根明确提出至少两次各25个基点的加
息。数据端也不弱，10月ISM制造业PMI 55.5、高于预期的54.6并连续10个月扩张，11月4日公布的10月
ADP新增8.6万人、高于预期，ISM非制造业54.5，9月JOLTS职位空缺752.3万高于预期，初请失业金连续四
周处在57年来低位。强数据没有把加息定价继续往上推，说明本周市场的注意力已经转到政治约束与后续通

胀路径上。12月加息仍是基准情景，本周定价在七成上下反复。
本周美联储12月加息预期变动不大

11月6日公布的10月非农新增16.5万人，市场预期集中在6万上下，实际值高出所有调查口径，也远高于过去
12个月4.8万的月均增量。失业率从4.2%降到4.1%，劳动参与率升到61.9%，平均时薪环比0.4%、同比
3.1%，9月时薪同比上修至3.2%。8月9月两个月合计上修8.4万，近三个月月均新增回到13.7万。此前按9月
初值推演的就业降温，修正后已经站不住。新增岗位里专业和商业服务增7.0万，过去12个月月均只有1.5
万，医疗保健4.2万、运输仓储2.5万、制造业1.9万，政府部门0.6万，分布比前几个月更宽。
这份读数把中期选举后被压低的利率链拉了回来。选举落地后，油价回落压低输入性通胀预期，AI监管担忧
又让市场下修增长与加息预期，强就业给12月加息补上依据，政策利率定价重新上修。市场整体反应偏平
淡，目前美联储关注重心在通胀数据，市场在等待下周的CPI结果。另外，移民收紧与退休增加让劳动力供给

### Page 4

收缩，维持失业率稳定所需的月增岗位已明显低于前几年，同样一份就业数据现在更容易转成工资压力。薪
资方面，时薪环比虽然超预期，但同比3.1%仍低于通胀，实际工资在降，这与中期选举里生活成本成为主要
议题对得上。12月会议前还有通胀报告，油价若继续回落，或者AI资产调整改变金融条件，加息的理由都会
被削弱。
1.3　中东局势缓和，但谈判尚无实质进展
本周中东局势从对抗转入谈判，但进展有限。11月3日伊朗提出在条件满足后两天内重开霍尔木兹海峡，特朗
普态度软化，表示中期选举后不打伊朗。11月5日伊朗革命卫队通报霍尔木兹附近两艘油轮与一艘拖船起火，
波斯湾近期至少5艘船只报告遇袭；截至目前，卡塔尔斡旋仍在进行，双方都称分歧很大，到本周结束没有正
式停火文本。
国会对战争的实际影响是财政战争拨款，众议院拨款可以在拨款法案里附加条款，限制或禁止联邦资金用于
对伊军事行动，参议院掌握外交与国防人事的确认，监督听证也能把军事目标与行动成本摆到台面上。总统
仍可否决这类法案，推翻否决需要两院各三分之二，民主党这次没到这个门槛，约束更可能落在追加战争经

从选举日到新国会就职这两个月，特朗普权限并不受限，既有动力尽快谈出一个结果，也可能在就职前升级

第二章 大类资产配置展望与建议
这些宏观事件体现在资产上影响路径并不单一。我们回顾1974到2022年的13次中期选举，标普500选后180
天13次全涨，平均约12.4%，在野党控制两院的年份平均涨幅更高。黄金在选后180天平均涨5.7%、胜率
77%；在选后60天平均涨2.7%、胜率62%，在野党控制两院时表现较弱。10年期美债收益率在选后30天下
行概率69%，平均下13.8BP，在野党控制的七次，下行概率100%、幅度更大。这套统计背后的驱动逻辑不
复杂。分裂政府意味着大额财政计划和减税推不动，国债新增供给减少，长端利率上行的推动力变小，美债
和黄金的逻辑一致。另一条逻辑通常是不确定性出清，选举结果落地，政策走向变清楚，风险资产的风险溢
价收窄。这条逻辑不依赖具体议题，历次中期选举都成立。但这次和以前有几处不同，不能直接照搬，我们

分资产进行分析：
权益方面，近期A股迎来超跌反弹，主要是情绪和资金驱动，难言反转。中期选举影响股市有好几条路径，方
向并不单一。对伊行动提前收尾的预期压低油价，通胀预期跟着下行。美联储加息预期回落带动美债收益率
走低，权益估值的分母改善，高估值成长受益最直接。对华方面，市场开始评估美国对中国科技封锁会不会
减弱，若松动，中概与国内科技资产的外部约束会边际缓和，但落地概率不高，目前更多是叙事层面。AI监
管趋严则方向相反，民主党主导国会后数据中心审批落到州与地方，AI监管趋严压住美股硬件链的盈利与估
值，再经情绪传到A股成长。国内两项稳市工具压低了资金成本，对指数进行托底。目前的上涨没有基本面配
合，修复行情能否延续，取决于成交额能否持续放量与超调买盘的持续性，后续美股科技股回调和非农强劲
带动加息预期升温也会压制风险偏好。
中债方面，中债这周偏强，靠的是避险资金和配置盘，超长端已经偏挤。信用风险冲击上周五引发的抛压在
这周迅速回补，债市情绪整体偏强。短期资产荒逻辑、宽松的资金面和避险情绪继续支撑债市。但目前债市

利率点位已经很低，加上监管多次提示长端风险，不建议追多；若后续股市走出趋势性修复，债市将承压。
美债方面，近期收益率明显下行，一方面是油价暴跌压低了输入性通胀预期，市场据此下调加息预期押注；
另一方面，中期选举后财政扩张预期下修，长端供给压力跟着缓解，期限溢价同步回落。但财政纪律能否真
正收紧还未可知，具体要看政策法案的推进。目前中长期财政压力和通胀风险并未解除，且美联储加息周期
尚未结束，短期的情绪性利多很难推动收益率持续下行。
大宗商品方面，能化的核心主线仍是中东局势，上半周和谈预期升温推动油价快速下行，但和谈并无实质进
展加上一些海峡摩擦仍存，目前油价已经开始反弹，若谈判破裂则油价会继续快速走升。选举结果对油价的
传导偏慢，虽然短期消息面和谈预期升温，但不能排除特朗普在新国会就任之前开启新一轮打击的可能。贵
金属本周明显反弹，来自油价、美元与美债收益率同步回落、加息定价松动。但继续向上突破需要更多催化

因素。短期预计仍是区间震荡行情，后续等待紧缩预期见顶或地缘缓和信号。其余品种方向不清晰，暂不展
